&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\big|\xi_{\hat{P}}-\xi_{\hat{Q}}\big|+\big|\eta_{\hat{P}}-\eta_{\hat{Q}}\big|\Big)+\mathcal{C}m^{(\rm h)}\big(2\epsilon_{I}+\tilde{\epsilon}\big)\big|\xi_{\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})}-\xi_{\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})}\big|,\end{split}

(4.100)

where
\mathcal{C}
is a positive constant depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
.
Therefore, by choosing constants still denoted by
\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,3}>0
and
\epsilon_{\rm h,3}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
, such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small and
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,3}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{\rm h,3})
, we have
\mathcal{C}m^{(\rm h)}\big(2\epsilon_{I}+\tilde{\epsilon}\big)<\frac{1}{2}
, which implies that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big|\xi_{\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})}-\xi_{\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})}\big|\leq 2\mathcal{C}\Big(\big|\xi_{\hat{P}}-\xi_{\hat{Q}}\big|+\big|\eta_{\hat{P}}-\eta_{\hat{Q}}\big|\Big).\end{split}

(4.101)

Hence, the estimate (i) in (
4.96
) follows from the estimates (
4.99
) and (
4.101
).

Next, by taking
\eta_{\hat{P}}=\eta_{\hat{Q}}
in (
4.100
), the estimate (ii) in (
4.96
) follows from the estimates
(
4.99
) and (
4.101
).

Finally, by (
4.94
) with
k=\hat{Q}
, and the estimates (
4.85
) and (
4.98
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}d\big(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}),\hat{Q}\big)&=\big|\eta_{\hat{P}}-\eta_{\hat{Q}}\big|+\bigg|\int^{\eta_{\hat{Q}}}_{\eta_{\hat{P}}}\frac{d\tau}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\big(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau)\big)}\bigg|\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\big|\eta_{\hat{P}}-\eta_{\hat{Q}}\big|,\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}d\big(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}),\hat{Q}\big)&=\big|\eta_{\hat{P}}-\eta_{\hat{Q}}\big|+\bigg|\int^{\eta_{\hat{Q}}}_{\eta_{\hat{P}}}\frac{d\tau}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\big(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau)\big)}\bigg|\\[5.0pt]
&\geq\mathcal{C}^{-1}\big|\eta_{\hat{P}}-\eta_{\hat{Q}}\big|,\end{split}

which gives (iii) in (
4.96
). The proof is completed.
∎

Based on the definition, for any point
(\xi,\eta)
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cap\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}
, there exists a unique
\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}
,
such that the characteristics corresponding to
\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}
issuing from
(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(h)})
passes through
(\xi,\eta)
.
Hence, by (
4.98
) and implicit function theorem, we can regard
\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}
as a function of
(\xi,\eta)
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cap\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}
,
i.e.
,
\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}=\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\xi,\eta)
.
We have the following lemma.

Lemma 4.4
.

For any
\alpha\in(0,1)
and
\delta z^{(\rm n-1)}\in\mathscr{K}_{2\epsilon_{I}}
,
there exist constants
\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,4}>0
and
\epsilon_{\rm h,4}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
,
such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small and
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,4}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{I,4})
, one has

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}\leq\hat{C}_{44},\end{split}

(4.102)

where the constant
\hat{C}_{44}=\hat{C}_{44}(\underline{\tilde{U}},L,\alpha)>0
is independent of
\epsilon_{I}
and
\rm n
.

Proof.

By choosing the constants
\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,4}>0
and
\epsilon_{\rm h,4}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
,
such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,4}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{\rm h,4})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small,
the estimate for
\|D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cap\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}
follows easily as done in
[
36
, Lemma 4.2]
.
Next, let us consider
[D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}]_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cap\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}
.
By
(
4.93
)
, the straightforward computation shows that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{\partial\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}{\partial\xi}=\frac{1}{1+\int^{\eta}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)e^{\int^{\tau}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)ds}d\tau},\\[5.0pt]
&\frac{\partial\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}{\partial\eta}=-\frac{1}{\Big(1+\int^{\eta}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\big)e^{\int^{\tau}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\big)ds}d\tau\Big)\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}.\end{split}

For the two points
\hat{P}=(\xi_{\hat{P}},\eta_{\hat{P}}),\ \hat{Q}=(\xi_{\hat{Q}},\eta_{\hat{Q}})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}
,
there exist unique points
\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})=(\xi_{\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})},-m^{(\rm h)})=(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{P}),-m^{(\rm h)})
and
\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})=(\xi_{\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})},-m^{(\rm h)})=(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q}),-m^{(\rm h)})
so that the characteristics determined by
\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}
issuing from them pass through
\hat{P}
and
\hat{Q}
, respectively.
Direct computation yields that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&d^{-\alpha}(\hat{P},\hat{Q})\Big|D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{P})-D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q})\Big|\\[5.0pt]
\leq&d^{-\alpha}(\hat{P},\hat{Q})\Big|\partial_{\xi}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{P})-\partial_{\xi}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q})\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\ +d^{-\alpha}(\hat{P},\hat{Q})\Big|\partial_{\eta}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{P})-\partial_{\eta}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q})\Big|\\[5.0pt]
=:&\hat{I}_{1}+\hat{I}_{2}.\end{split}

For
\hat{I}_{1}
, by (
4.94
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\hat{I}_{1}=d^{-\alpha}(\hat{P},\hat{Q})\big|\partial_{\xi}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{P})-\partial_{\xi}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q})\big|\\[5.0pt]
=&\frac{d^{-\alpha}(\hat{P},\hat{Q})\Big|\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)e^{\int^{\tau}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)ds}d\tau-\int^{\eta_{\hat{Q}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)e^{\int^{\tau}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)ds}d\tau\Big|}{\bigg|\Big(1+\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)e^{\int^{\tau}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)ds}d\tau\Big)\Big(1+\int^{\eta_{\hat{Q}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)e^{\int^{\tau}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)ds}d\tau\Big)\bigg|}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\mathcal{C}d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P},\hat{Q}\big)\Bigg\{\bigg|\int^{\eta_{\hat{Q}}}_{\eta_{\hat{P}}}\partial_{\xi}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau))}\Big)e^{\int^{\tau}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)ds}d\tau\bigg|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad+\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\bigg|\partial_{\xi}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}(\hat{P}_{+}(\tau))}\Big)e^{\int^{\tau}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)ds}-\partial_{\xi}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau))}\Big)e^{\int^{\tau}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)ds}\bigg|d\tau\Bigg\}.\end{split}

Then, we can get, for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,4}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{\rm h,4})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hat{I}_{1}\leq\mathcal{C}\big|\eta_{\hat{P}}-\eta_{\hat{Q}}\big|^{1-\alpha}+\mathcal{C}m^{(\rm h)}\bigg(\big\|D\delta z^{(\rm n-1)}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\big\|\big(\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm h)^{\prime}}_{0},\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm h)^{\prime}}_{0}\big)\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}\bigg)\leq\mathcal{C},\end{split}

by (
4.97
) and by choosing constants
\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,4}>0
and
\epsilon_{\rm h,4}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
. Similarly, one can also get
\hat{I}_{2}\leq\mathcal{C}
. We therefore have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&[D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}]_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}\\[5.0pt]
=&\sup_{\hat{P},\hat{Q}\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}d^{-\alpha}(\hat{P},\hat{Q})\Big|D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{P})-D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q})\Big|\\[5.0pt]
\quad&\leq\mathcal{C}.\end{split}

This completes the proof of this lemma.
∎

Now we consider the characteristics issuing from the transonic contact discontinuity
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}
. Let
\xi=\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta,\xi_{\rm cd,0})
be the characteristic curves corresponding to

\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}
, and issuing from point
(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)
, where
\xi_{\rm cd,0}\in[0,\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}]
and
\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}
is given by (
4.91
), i.e., they are defined by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\frac{d\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}}{d\eta}=\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta,\xi_{\rm cd,0}),\eta)},\\[5.0pt]
\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(0,\xi_{\rm cd,0})=\xi_{\rm cd,0},\end{array}\right.\end{split}

(4.103)

where
\xi_{\rm cd,0}\in[0,\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}]
. Then,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\xi=\xi_{\rm cd,0}+\int^{\eta}_{0}\frac{d\tau}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\tau,\xi_{\rm cd,0}),\tau)}.\end{split}

(4.104)

For any two points
\check{P}=(\xi_{\check{P}},\eta_{\check{P}}),\ \check{Q}=(\xi_{\check{Q}},\eta_{\check{Q}})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}
, there exist unique points
\check{P}_{-}(0)=(\xi_{\check{P}_{-}(0)},0),\ \check{Q}_{-}(0)=(\xi_{\check{Q}_{-}(0)},0)
so that the curve

\xi=\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta,\xi_{\rm cd,0})
issuing from
them pass through
\check{P}
and
\check{Q}
, respectively. Define
\check{P}_{-}(\eta)=(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta,\xi_{\check{P}_{-}(0)}),\eta)

and
\check{Q}_{-}(\eta)=(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta,\xi_{\check{Q}_{-}(0)}),\eta)
, then

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\xi_{\check{P}_{-}(\eta)}=\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta,\xi_{\check{P}_{-}(0)})=\xi_{\check{P}_{-}(0)}+\int^{\eta}_{0}\frac{d\tau}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}(\check{P}_{-}(\tau))},\\[5.0pt]
&\xi_{\check{Q}_{-}(\eta)}=\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta,\xi_{\check{Q}_{-}(0)})=\xi_{\check{Q}_{-}(0)}+\int^{\eta}_{0}\frac{d\tau}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}(\check{Q}_{-}(\tau))}.\end{split}

(4.105)

Define

\displaystyle\begin{split}d\big(\check{P}_{-}(\eta),\check{Q}_{-}(\eta)\big)=\big|\check{P}_{-}(\eta)-\check{Q}_{-}(\eta)\big|,\ \ d^{\alpha}\big(\check{P}_{-}(\eta),\check{Q}_{-}(\eta)\big)=\big|\check{P}_{-}(\eta)-\check{Q}_{-}(\eta)\big|^{\alpha},\ \forall\alpha\in(0,1).\end{split}

(4.106)

Then, we have the following lemma, the proof of which is analogous to that of Lemma
4.3
and thus omitted.

Lemma 4.5
.

For any
\alpha\in(0,1)
and
\delta z^{(\rm n-1)}\in\mathscr{K}_{2\epsilon_{I}}
,
there exist constants
\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,5}>0
and
\epsilon_{\rm h,5}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
,
such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small and
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,5}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{I,5})
, the following holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}(\rm a)\ \ &d^{\alpha}\big(\check{P}_{-}(\eta),\check{Q}_{-}(\eta)\big)\leq\check{C}_{41}d^{\alpha}\big(\check{P},\check{Q}\big),\quad&\eta\in[0,\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}}],\\[5.0pt]
(\rm b)\ \ &d^{\alpha}\big(\check{P}_{-}(\eta),\check{Q}_{-}(\eta)\big)\leq\check{C}_{42}d^{\alpha}\big(\check{P}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}}),\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}})\big),\quad&\eta\in[0,\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}}],\\[5.0pt]
(\rm c)\ \ &{\check{C}^{-1}_{43}\big|\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}}-\eta_{\check{Q}}\big|^{\alpha}\leq d^{\alpha}\big(\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}}),\check{Q}\big)\leq\check{C}_{43}\big|\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}}-\eta_{\check{Q}}\big|^{\alpha},}\end{split}

(4.107)

where
the constants
\check{C}_{4j}>0,(j=1,2,3)
depend only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
,
and
\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}}=\min\{\eta_{\check{P}},\ \eta_{\check{Q}}\}
and
\check{P}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}\hat{Q}})=\big(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}},\xi_{\check{P}_{-}(0)}),\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}}\big)
and
\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}})=\big(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}},\xi_{\check{Q}_{-}(0)}),\eta_{\check{P}\check{Q}}\big)
.

Finally, for any point
(\xi,\eta)
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}
,
there exists a unique
\xi_{\rm cd,0}
, such that the characteristics corresponding to
\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}
issuing from
(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)
passes through
(\xi,\eta)
.
So we can regard
\xi_{\rm cd,0}
as a function of
(\xi,\eta)
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}
,
i.e.
,

\xi_{\rm cd,0}=\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\xi,\eta)
.
Then, we also have the following lemma, the proof of which is similar to that of Lemma
4.4
and thus omitted as well.

Lemma 4.6
.

For any
\alpha\in(0,1)
and
\delta z^{(\rm n-1)}\in\mathscr{K}_{2\epsilon_{I}}
,
there exist constants
\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,6}>0
and
\epsilon_{\rm h,6}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
,
such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small and
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,6}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{\rm h,6})
, one has

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|D\xi_{\rm cd,0}\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}\leq\check{C}_{44},\end{split}

(4.108)

where the constant
\check{C}_{44}>0
depends only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
.

4.2.2.
Estimates of the solutions to the problem
(\widetilde{\mathbf{FP}})_{\rm n}
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}

In this subsection, we will consider the estimate of solutions in the supersonic region
\Omega^{(\rm h)}
for the problem
(\widetilde{\mathbf{FP}})_{\rm n}

near the background state
\underline{z}
, which can be written as the following:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(n)}_{-}+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}=0,&\qquad\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}=0,&\qquad\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III},\\[5.0pt]
\delta z^{(\rm n)}=\delta z_{0},&\qquad\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}.\end{array}\right.\end{split}

(4.109)

Our main result in this subsection can be stated below.

Proposition 4.3
.

For any given
\alpha\in(0,1)
,
there exist constants
\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,7}>0
and
\epsilon_{\rm h,7}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
,
such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,7}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{\rm h,7})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small,
if
\delta z^{(\rm n-1)}\in\mathscr{K}_{2\epsilon_{I}}
,
the solution
\delta z^{(\rm n)}
of the problem (
4.109
) satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}}+\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}}\leq\tilde{C}^{*}_{41}\Big(\|\delta z_{-,0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}+\|\delta z_{+,0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}\Big),\end{split}

(4.110)

where
\tilde{C}^{*}_{41}>0
depends only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
.

Proof.

We only consider the estimate for the solution
z^{(\rm n)}_{-}
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}
,
because the proof for
z^{(\rm n)}_{+}
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}
is similar.

First, for any point
(\xi,\eta)\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}
, we can draw a characteristic line
\eta=\phi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi,\eta_{+,0})

defined by (
4.77
)-(
4.78
) such that it intersects with
\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}
at the point
(0,\eta_{+,0})
. Then, one has
\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi,\eta)=\delta z_{-,0}(\eta_{+,0})
. Moreover, differentiating the equation
\eqref{eq:4.42}_{1}
with respect to
\xi
and
\eta
, and then integrating them along the characteristics
\eta=\phi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi,\eta_{+,0})
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi,\eta)&=\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(0,\eta_{+,0})-\int^{\xi}_{0}\big(\partial_{\tau}\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)(\tau,\phi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\tau,\eta_{+,0}))d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&=\partial_{\xi}\eta_{+,0}\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(0,\eta_{+,0})-\int^{\xi}_{0}\big(\partial_{\tau}\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)(\tau,\phi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\tau,\eta_{+,0}))d\tau,\end{split}

(4.111)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi,\eta)&=\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(0,\eta_{+,0})-\int^{\xi}_{0}\big(\partial_{\eta}\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)(\tau,\phi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\tau,\eta_{+,0}))d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&=\partial_{\eta}\eta_{+,0}\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(0,\eta_{+,0})-\int^{\xi}_{0}\big(\partial_{\eta}\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)(\tau,\phi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\tau,\eta_{+,0}))d\tau.\end{split}

(4.112)

Therefore, we can follow the approach in
[
36
]
for proving the Proposition 4.1 and apply the Lemma
4.2
as well as the estimate (
4.85
) to get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}}\leq 2\mathcal{C}\big\|\delta z_{-,0}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}},\end{split}

(4.113)

provided that
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{\rm h,7}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime}_{\rm h,7})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
is sufficiently small, where the constants
\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{\rm h,7}>\max\{\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,1},\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,2}\}
,
0<\epsilon^{\prime}_{\rm h,7}<\min\{\epsilon_{\rm h,1},\epsilon_{\rm h,2}\}
and

\mathcal{C}>0
depends only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
.

Next, let us turn to the estimate of
[D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}]_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}\cap(\tilde{\Omega}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}_{II})}
.
For the two points
P=(\xi_{P},\eta_{P})
,
Q=(\xi_{Q},\eta_{Q})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}
lying on the
characteristics corresponding to
\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}
and intersecting with
\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}
at
the points
P_{+}(0)=(0,\eta_{P_{+}(0)})
and
Q_{+}(0)=(0,\eta_{Q_{+}(0)})
, respectively,
by (
4.111
)-(
4.112
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P)&=\partial_{\xi}\eta_{+,0}(P)\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(P_{+}(0))-\int^{\xi_{P}}_{0}\big(\partial_{\tau}\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)(P_{+}(\tau))d\tau,\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P)&=\partial_{\eta}\eta_{+,0}(P)\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(P_{+}(0))-\int^{\xi_{P}}_{0}\big(\partial_{\eta}\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)(P_{+}(\tau))d\tau,\end{split}

(4.114)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q)&=\partial_{\xi}\eta_{+,0}(Q)\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(Q_{+}(0))-\int^{\xi_{Q}}_{0}\big(\partial_{\tau}\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)(Q_{+}(\tau))d\tau,\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q)&=\partial_{\eta}\eta_{+,0}(Q)\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(Q_{+}(0))-\int^{\xi_{Q}}_{0}\big(\partial_{\eta}\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)(Q_{+}(\tau))d\tau,\end{split}

(4.115)

where
\eta_{P_{+}(0)}=\eta_{+,0}(P)
and
\eta_{Q_{+}(0)}=\eta_{+,0}(Q)
.

Without loss of the generality, we asume that
\xi_{P}<\xi_{Q}
and
let
Q_{+}(\xi_{P})=(\xi_{P},\phi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi_{P},\eta_{Q_{+}(0)}))
.
Then, by a direct computation and by Lemma
4.1
, one can get that

\displaystyle d^{-\alpha}(P,Q)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P)-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q)\Big|

\displaystyle\quad\ \ \leq d^{-\alpha}(P,Q)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P)-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))\Big|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\ \ +d^{-\alpha}(P,Q)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q)\Big|

(4.116)

\displaystyle\quad\ \ \leq\mathcal{C}d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P)-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))\Big|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\ \ +\mathcal{C}d^{-\alpha}\big(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}),Q\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q)\Big|

\displaystyle\quad\ \ =:\mathcal{C}(J_{1}+J_{2}),

where constant
\mathcal{C}>0
depends only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
.

Now, we will estimate
J_{1}
and
J_{2}
. For the term
J_{1}
, by (
4.114
) and (
4.111
)-(
4.112
) for
Q_{+}(\xi_{p})
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{1}&\leq d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\Big|D\eta_{+,0}(P)\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(P_{+}(0))-D\eta_{+,0}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(Q_{+}(0))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ +d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\int^{\xi_{P}}_{0}\Big|\big(D\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\tau}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)(P_{+}(\tau))-\big(D\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\tau}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)(Q_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&=:J_{11}+J_{12}.\end{split}

For
J_{11}
, it follows from Lemma
4.1
and Lemma
4.2
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{11}&=d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\Big|D\eta_{+,0}(P)\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(P_{+}(0))-D\eta_{+,0}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(Q_{+}(0))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\leq d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\Big|D\eta_{+,0}(P)-D\eta_{+,0}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))\Big|\big|\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(P_{+}(0))\big|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ +d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\Big|\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(P_{+}(0))-\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(Q_{+}(0))\Big|\big|D\eta_{0,+}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))\big|\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\big|\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(P_{+}(0))\big|d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\Big|D\eta_{+,0}(P)-D\eta_{+,0}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ +\mathcal{C}\big|D\eta_{+,0}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))\big|d^{-\alpha}\big(P_{+}(0),Q_{+}(0)\big)\Big|\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(P_{+}(0))-\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(Q_{+}(0))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\big\|\delta z^{\prime}_{-,0}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}},\end{split}

where
\mathcal{C}=\mathcal{C}(\tilde{\underline{U}},L,\alpha)>0
is independent of
\epsilon_{I}
and
\rm n
.
Next, for
J_{12}
, a direct computation shows that

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{12}&=d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\int^{\xi_{P}}_{0}\Big|\Big(D\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\tau}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\Big)(P_{+}(\tau))-\Big(D\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\tau}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\Big)(Q_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&\leq d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\int^{\xi_{P}}_{0}\Big|\Big(D\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}(P_{+}(\tau))-D\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}(Q_{+}(\tau))\Big)\partial_{\tau}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ +d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\int^{\xi_{P}}_{0}\Big|\Big(\partial_{\tau}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P_{+}(\tau))-\partial_{\tau}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\tau))\Big)D\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}(Q_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau.\end{split}

Then, by the estimates (
4.85
) and (
4.113
), we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{12}&\leq\mathcal{C}\|\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\int^{\xi_{P}}_{0}\big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P(\tau))\big|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ +\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n-1)}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\big\|\big(\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm h)^{\prime}}_{0},\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm h)^{\prime}}_{0}\big)\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\quad\ \times\int^{\xi_{P}}_{0}d^{-\alpha}\big(P_{+}(\tau),Q_{+}(\tau)\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P_{+}(\tau))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}{\big\|\delta z_{-,0}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}}+\mathcal{C}\big(2\epsilon_{I}+\tilde{\epsilon}\big)\int^{\xi_{P}}_{0}d^{-\alpha}\big(P_{+}(\tau),Q_{+}(\tau)\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P_{+}(\tau))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau,\end{split}

where
\mathcal{C}=\mathcal{C}(\tilde{\underline{U}},L,\alpha)>0
is independent of
\epsilon_{I}
and
\rm n
.

Combining the estimates on
J_{11}
with
J_{12}
, we can deduce by the Gronwall inequality that

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{1}=d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P)-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))\big|\leq\mathcal{C}\big\|\delta z^{\prime}_{-,0}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}e^{2\mathcal{C}(2\epsilon_{I}+\tilde{\epsilon})\xi_{P}},\end{split}

which implies

\begin{split}J_{1}&\leq\sup_{P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}}d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P)-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\leq 2\mathcal{C}{\big\|\delta z_{-,0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}}.\end{split}

(4.117)

Here, we also choose the constants
\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,7}>0
and
\epsilon^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,7}>0
depends only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
, such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small and
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,7}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,7})
,
it holds
e^{2\mathcal{C}L(2\epsilon_{I}+\tilde{\epsilon})}\leq 2
.

The remaining task is to deal with
J_{2}
.
By the equations in (
4.115
), (
4.111
)-(
4.112
) for
Q_{+}(\xi_{p})
and the estimate (
4.85
), and Lemmas
4.1
-
4.2
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{2}&=d^{-\alpha}\big(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}),Q\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q)\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\leq d^{-\alpha}\big(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}),Q\big)\Big|D\eta_{+,0}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))-D\eta_{+,0}(Q)\Big||\partial_{\eta_{+,0}}\delta z_{-}(Q_{+}(0))|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad+d^{-\alpha}\big(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}),Q\big)\int^{\xi_{Q}}_{\xi_{P}}\Big|\big(D\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)(Q_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|D\eta_{+,0}\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}}\big\|\delta z^{\prime}_{-,0}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}+\big|\xi_{P}-\xi_{Q}\big|^{1-\alpha}\big\|\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n-1)}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\big\|\delta z_{-,0}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}.\end{split}

(4.118)

Combining all the estimates from (
4.116
) to (
4.118
) above together,
we therefore get that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&[D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}]_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}}\\[5.0pt]
=&\sup_{P,Q\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}}d^{-\alpha}(P,Q)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P)-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q)\Big|\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\mathcal{C}\sup_{P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}}d^{-\alpha}\big(P,Q_{+}(\xi_{P})\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(P)-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad+\sup_{Q_{+}(\xi_{P}),Q\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}}d^{-\alpha}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}),Q\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q_{+}(\xi_{P}))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(Q)\Big|\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\mathcal{C}\big\|\delta z^{\prime}_{-,0}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}},\end{split}

(4.119)

where the constant
\mathcal{C}>0
depends only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
.

Finally, it follows from the estimates (
4.113
) and (
4.119
) that we can choose the constants
\tilde{C}^{*}_{41}>0
,
\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,7}=\max\{\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{\rm h,7},\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,7}\}
and

\epsilon_{\rm h,7}=\min\{\epsilon^{\prime}_{\rm h,7},\epsilon^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,7}\}
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha

such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,7}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{\rm h,7})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small,
it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}}\leq\tilde{C}^{*}_{41}\|\delta z_{-,0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}.\end{split}

The proof of this proposition is completed.
∎

Remark 4.2
.

By the estimate (
4.110
), we actually have the estimates of
\delta z_{\pm}^{(\rm n)}
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{I}
. Moreover, on the contact discontinuity
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}

and the lower wall of the nozzle
\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}
, we also have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}}+\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}}\leq\tilde{C}^{*}_{42}\bigg(\big\|\delta z_{-,0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}+\big\|\delta z_{+,0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}\bigg).\end{split}

(4.120)

4.2.3.
Estimates of the solutions to the problem
(\widetilde{\mathbf{FP}})_{\rm n}
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}

Problem
(\widetilde{\mathbf{FP}})_{\rm n}
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}
can be formulated as the following:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}=0,&\qquad\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V},\\[5.0pt]
\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}+\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}=2\arctan g^{\prime}_{-},&\qquad\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}.\end{array}\right.\end{split}

(4.121)

Similar to the argument in Section 4.2.2, we also study problem (
4.121
) via the characteristics method, but the difference is that there are reflections of the characteristics on the lower wall
\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}
of the nozzle. Let
\eta=\chi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi,\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0})
be the reflected characteristics which issues from
(\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(\rm h)})
and
intersects with the transonic contact discontinuity
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}
at the point
(\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,1},0)
. Then, it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\int^{0}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\frac{d\tau}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\tau,\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}),\tau)}=\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,1}.\end{split}

(4.122)

We have the following estimates for
\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}
.

Proposition 4.4
.

For any given
\alpha\in(0,1)
,
there exist constants
\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,8}>0
and
\epsilon_{\rm h,8}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
,
such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,8}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{\rm h,8})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small, if
\delta z^{(\rm n-1)}\in\mathscr{K}_{2\epsilon_{I}}
,

the solution
z^{(\rm n)}_{-}
to the problem (
4.121
) satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\leq\tilde{C}^{*}_{43}\bigg(\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}+\big\|g_{-}+1\big\|_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\bigg),\end{split}

(4.123)

where
\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}
is given in (
4.91
) and the constant
\tilde{C}^{*}_{43}>0
depends only on
\underline{\tilde{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
.

Proof.

By
\eqref{eq:4.70}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}+\frac{1}{{\lambda}^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}=0.\end{split}

(4.124)

For any point
(\xi,\eta)\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}
, we can define a characteristic line

\xi=\chi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\eta,\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0})
by (
4.92
)-(
4.93
) such that it intersects with the boundary
\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}
at the point
(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(\rm h)})
.
Then, by the boundary condition on
\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}
,
we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi,\eta)=\delta z_{-}(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(\rm h)})=2\arctan g^{\prime}_{-}(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0})-\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(\rm h)}).\end{split}

(4.125)

Next, differentiating the equation
(
4.124
)
with respect to
\xi
and
\eta
, and then integrating them along the characteristics
\xi=\chi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\eta,\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0})
, we obatin

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi,\eta)\\[5.0pt]
&=\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(\rm h)})-\int^{\eta}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\tau,\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}),\tau\big)d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&=\partial_{\xi}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(\rm h)})-\int^{\eta}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\tau,\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}),\tau\big)d\tau,\end{split}

(4.126)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi,\eta)\\[5.0pt]
&=\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(\rm h)})-\int^{\eta}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\tau}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\tau,\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}),\tau\big)d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&=\partial_{\eta}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(\rm h)})-\int^{\eta}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\tau}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\tau,\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}),\tau\big)d\tau.\end{split}

(4.127)

By using the boundary condition
\eqref{eq:4.70}_{2}
on
\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}
, we further have for

\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(\rm h)})
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(\rm h)})=\frac{2g^{\prime\prime}_{-}}{1+(g^{\prime}_{-})^{2}}-\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0},-m^{(\rm h)}).\end{split}

(4.128)

Then, following the same argument as in
[
36
]
for the proof of Proposition
4.2
and by Lemma
4.4
,
there exist constants
\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{\rm h,8}>\max\{\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,3},\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,4}\}
,
\epsilon^{\prime}_{\rm h,8}<\min\{\epsilon_{\rm h,3},\epsilon_{\rm h,4}\}
and

\mathcal{C}>0
depending only on

\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
, such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{\rm h,8}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime}_{\rm h,8})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}+\big\|g^{\prime}_{-}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}\Big).\end{split}

(4.129)

Therefore, it remains to obtain the estimate of
\big[D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big]_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}
. For the given two points
\hat{P}=(\xi_{\hat{P}},\eta_{\hat{P}}),\ \hat{Q}=(\xi_{\hat{Q}},\eta_{\hat{Q}})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}
, satisfying (
4.94
) with
\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})=(\xi_{\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})},-m^{(\rm h)})
and
\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})=(\xi_{\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})},-m^{(\rm h)})
,
substituting them into (
4.126
)-(
4.127
), we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P})&=\partial_{\xi}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{P})\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad-\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big(\hat{P}_{+}(\tau)\big)d\tau,\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P})&=\partial_{\eta}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{P})\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad-\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\tau}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big(\hat{P}_{+}(\tau)\big)d\tau,\end{split}

(4.130)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q})&=\partial_{\xi}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q})\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad-\int^{\eta_{\hat{Q}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\xi}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau)\big)d\tau,\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q})&=\partial_{\eta}\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q})\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad-\int^{\eta_{\hat{Q}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\partial_{\tau}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau)\big)d\tau.\end{split}

(4.131)

Similarly to the arguments in proving the Proposition
4.3
, we first derive the estimate for

d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P},\hat{Q}\big)\big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P})-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q})\big|
.
To this end, we assume that
\eta_{\hat{P}}<\eta_{\hat{Q}}

and let
\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}})=(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}},\xi_{\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})}),\eta_{\hat{P}})
, and the other cases can be treated in the same way.
By (
4.130
) and (
4.127
)–(
4.128
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&d^{-\alpha}(\hat{P},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P})-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
\leq&d^{-\alpha}(\hat{P},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))\Big|D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{P})\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad-D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&+d^{-\alpha}(\hat{P},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\bigg|\Big(D\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\Big)(\hat{P}_{+}(\tau))-\Big(D\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\Big)(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau))\bigg|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&=:\hat{J}_{1}+\hat{J}_{2}.\end{split}

(4.132)

For
\hat{J}_{1}
, by Lemma
4.3
and Lemma
4.4
, one has

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hat{J}_{1}&\leq d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}})\big)\Big|D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{P})-D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))\Big|\big|\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\big|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ +\mathcal{C}\big|D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))\big|d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}(-m^{(\rm h)}),\hat{Q}(-m^{(\rm h)})\big)\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\ \ \times\Big|\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))-\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\|D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}\big\|\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ +\mathcal{C}\big\|D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},1}\}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}(-m^{(\rm h)}),\hat{Q}(-m^{(\rm h)})\big)\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\ \ \times\Big|\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))-\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\big\|\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}+\mathcal{C}d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}(-m^{(\rm h)}),\hat{Q}(-m^{(\rm h)})\big)\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\Big|\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))-\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\Big|,\end{split}

(4.133)

where the constant
\mathcal{C}=\mathcal{C}(\tilde{\underline{U}},L,\alpha)>0
is independent of
\epsilon_{I}
and
\rm n
.
By (
4.128
), we know that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}\leq\big\|g^{\prime\prime}_{-}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}+\big\|\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}.\end{split}

For the last term in (
4.133
), by using (
4.128
) again and choosing the constants
\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8}>0
,
\epsilon^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8}>0
depending only on

\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
, such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
be sufficiently small,
we have

\displaystyle d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}(-m^{(\rm h)}),\hat{Q}(-m^{(\rm h)})\big)\Big|\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))-\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}(-m^{(\rm h)}),\hat{Q}(-m^{(\rm h)})\big)\Bigg|\frac{g^{\prime\prime}_{-}(\xi_{\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})})}{1+\big(g^{\prime}_{-}(\xi_{\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})})\big)^{2}}-\frac{g^{\prime\prime}_{-}(\xi_{\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})})}{1+\big(g^{\prime}_{-}(\xi_{\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})})\big)^{2}}\Bigg|

\displaystyle+d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}(-m^{(\rm h)}),\hat{Q}(-m^{(\rm h)})\big)\Big|\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))-\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)}))\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}\big(1+(g^{\prime}_{-})^{2}\big)d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}(-m^{(\rm h)}),\hat{Q}(-m^{(\rm h)})\big)\Big|g^{\prime\prime}_{-}(\xi_{\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})})-g^{\prime\prime}_{-}(\xi_{\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})})\Big|

\displaystyle+\mathcal{C}\big|g^{\prime\prime}_{-}\big|d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}(-m^{(\rm h)}),\hat{Q}(-m^{(\rm h)})\big)\Big|\big(g^{\prime}_{-}(\xi_{\hat{P}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})})\big)^{2}-\big(g^{\prime}_{-}(\xi_{\hat{Q}_{+}(-m^{(\rm h)})})\big)^{2}\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad+\big\|\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}+\big\|g^{\prime\prime}_{-}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}\Big),

where the constant
\mathcal{C}=\mathcal{C}(\tilde{\underline{U}},L,\alpha)>0
is independent of
\epsilon_{I}
and
\rm n
. Hence, substituting the above two estimates into (
4.133
), we obtain the estimate for
\hat{J}_{1}
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hat{J}_{1}&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}+\big\|g^{\prime\prime}_{-}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}\Big),\end{split}

(4.134)

where the constant
\mathcal{C}>0
depends only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
.

Let us consider
\hat{J}_{2}
now. By Lemma
4.3
, a direct computation yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hat{J}_{2}&\leq d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}})\big)\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\bigg|D\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)(\hat{P}_{+}(\tau))\bigg|\Big|\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}_{+}(\tau))-\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \ +d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}})\big)\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\bigg|D\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)(\hat{P}_{+}(\tau))-D\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau))\bigg|\Big|\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&=:\hat{J}_{21}+\hat{J}_{22}.\end{split}

For
\hat{J}_{21}
, by Lemma
4.3
and (
4.96
), there exists a constant
\mathcal{C}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha

such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hat{J}_{21}&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|\delta z^{(\rm n-1)}\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\big\|\big(\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm h)^{\prime}}_{0},\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm h)^{\prime}}_{0}\big)\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ \ \times\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}_{+}(\tau),\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau)\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}_{+}(\tau))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\big(2\epsilon_{I}+\tilde{\epsilon}\big)\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}_{+}(\tau),\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau)\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}_{+}(\tau))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau.\end{split}

For
\hat{J}_{22}
, by (
4.98
)(
4.129
) and Lemma
4.3
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hat{J}_{22}&\leq\mathcal{C}\big\|\big(\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\big)^{-1}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}+\big\|g^{\prime}_{-}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}\Big),\end{split}

where
\mathcal{C}>0
depends only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
. By (
4.134
) as well as the estimates for
\hat{J}_{21}
and
\hat{J}_{22}
,
we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}})\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P})-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-,0}\}}+\big\|g^{\prime}_{+}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-,0}\}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\ \ +\mathcal{C}\big(2\epsilon_{I}+\tilde{\epsilon}\big)\int^{\eta_{\hat{P}}}_{-m^{(\rm h)}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}(\tau),\hat{Q}(\tau)\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P}(\tau))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}(\tau))\Big|d\tau.\end{split}

Then it follows from the Gronwall inequality that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}})\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P})-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\quad\ \ \leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}+\big\|g^{\prime}_{+}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}\Big)e^{\mathcal{C}(2\epsilon_{I}+\tilde{\epsilon})m^{(\rm h)}}.\end{split}

Taking the constants
\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8}>0
,
\epsilon^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8}>0
depending only on

\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
, and letting
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
be sufficiently small such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8})
, one has

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{\hat{P},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P}_{+},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}})\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P})-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ \ \leq 2\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}+\big\|g^{\prime}_{+}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}\Big).\end{split}

(4.135)

On the other hand, by (
4.127
)–(
4.128
) and (
4.131
), we can also deduce that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\ d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}),\hat{Q}\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q})\Big|\\[5.0pt]
\leq&d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}),\hat{Q}\big)\big|D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))-D\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}(\hat{Q})\big||\partial_{\xi_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}}D\delta z_{-}(Q_{+}(0))|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad+d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}),\hat{Q}\big)\int^{\eta_{\hat{Q}}}_{\eta_{\hat{P}}}\Big|D\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\tau))\Big|d\tau,\end{split}

which implies by Lemma
4.3
and (
4.129
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}),\hat{Q}\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}),\hat{Q}\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q})\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}+\big\|g^{\prime\prime}_{+}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}\Big),\end{split}

provided that
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{\rm h,8}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime}_{\rm h,8})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
be sufficiently small.
Here, the constant
\mathcal{C}=\mathcal{C}(\tilde{\underline{U}},L,\alpha)>0
is independent of
\epsilon_{I}
and
\rm n
.
Combining (
4.134
) and (
4.135
) together, we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{\hat{P},\hat{Q}\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P},\hat{Q}\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P})-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q})\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \leq\sup_{\hat{P},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{P},\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}})\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{P})-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ \ \ +\sup_{\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}),\hat{Q}\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{III}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{V}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}),\hat{Q}\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q}_{+}(\eta_{\hat{P}}))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\hat{Q})\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}+\big\|g^{\prime}_{+}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}}\Big).\end{split}

(4.136)

Finally, by (
4.129
) and (
4.136
), we can choose constants
\tilde{C}^{*}_{44}>0
,
\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,8}=\max\{\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{\rm h,8},\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8},\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8}\}

and
\epsilon_{\rm h,8}=\min\{\epsilon^{\prime}_{\rm h,8},\epsilon^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8},\epsilon^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,8}\}
depending only on

\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,8}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{\rm h,8})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
be sufficiently small,
the inequality (
4.123
) holds. ∎

4.2.4.
Estimates of the solutions to the problem
(\widetilde{\mathbf{FP}})_{\rm n}
in the supersonic region
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}

Based on the results in Sections 4.1– 4.2.3, we will consider the problem
(\widetilde{\mathbf{FP}})_{\rm n}
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}
,
which involves the transonic contact discontinuity
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}
as well as the refection on it.
Problem

(\widetilde{\mathbf{FP}})_{\rm n}
in this region is

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}=0,&\ \mbox{in}\ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI},\\[5.0pt]
\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}=\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}-2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\partial_{\xi}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}-2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{\eta}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}-2c_{\rm cd}(\xi),&\ \mbox{on}\ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}.\end{array}\right.

Suppose that the characteristics
\xi=\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}})
issuing from the point
(\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0},0)
on
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}
intersects with the lower nozzle wall
\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}
at the point
(\xi^{*}_{-,1},-m^{(\rm h)})
. Then, it satisfies that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\int^{-m^{(\rm h)}}_{0}\frac{d\tau}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\tau,\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}),\tau)}=\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},1}.\end{split}

(4.140)

We first have the following proposition for
\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}
.

Proposition 4.5
.

For any given
\alpha\in(0,1)
and for

\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small
,
there exist constants
\tilde{C}^{*}_{46}>0
,
\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,9}>0
and
\epsilon_{\rm h,9}>0
depending only on
\underline{\tilde{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
,
such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,9}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{\rm h,9})
, if
(\delta\varphi^{(\rm n-1)},\delta z^{(\rm n-1)})\in\mathscr{K}_{2\epsilon_{I}}
,
the solution
z^{(\rm n)}_{+}
of the problem (
4.2.4
) satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\tilde{C}^{*}_{46}\bigg(\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\|_{2,\alpha;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}+\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ \ +\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\bigg),\end{split}

(4.141)

where
\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}
is defined by (
4.91
).

Proof.

We first rewrite the equation for
\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}
as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}+\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}=0,\end{split}

(4.142)

and still consider
\eta>0
, otherwise we can replace
\eta
by
-\eta
.

For any point
(\xi,\eta)\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}
, we can define a characteristic line

\xi=\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta,\xi_{\rm cd,0})
by (
4.103
) and (
4.104
) such that it intersects with the boundary
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}

at the point
(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)
. Then, by the boundary condition
\eqref{eq:4.97}_{2}
on
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}
, we can derive that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi,\eta)=\delta z_{+}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)&=\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)-2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\partial_{\xi}\xi_{\rm cd,0}\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\ \ -2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{\eta}\xi_{\rm cd,0}\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)-2c_{\rm cd}(\xi_{\rm cd,0}).\end{split}

(4.143)

Thus, by Lemma
4.6
, there exist positive constants
\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{\rm h,9}>\max\{\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,5},\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,6}\}
and
0<\epsilon^{\prime}_{\rm h,9}<\min\{\epsilon_{\rm h,5},\epsilon_{\rm h,6}\}
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
, such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{\rm h,9}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime}_{\rm h,9})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small, it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\\
&\leq\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}+2\|\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\partial_{\xi}\xi_{\rm cd,0}\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\|_{0,0;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\\[5.0pt]
&\quad+2\|\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{\eta}\xi_{\rm cd,0}\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\|_{0,0;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}+2\|c_{\rm cd}\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}+\|D\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\|_{0,0;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&\quad+\mathcal{C}\Big(\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\Big),\end{split}

(4.144)

where
\mathcal{C}=\mathcal{C}(\tilde{\underline{U}},L)>0
.

Next, differentiating the equation
(
4.142
)
with respect to
\xi
and
\eta
, and then integrating them along the characteristics
\xi=\chi^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\eta,\xi_{\rm cd,0})
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi,\eta)&=\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)-\int^{\eta}_{0}\partial_{\xi}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\tau,\xi_{\rm cd,0}),\tau\big)d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&=\partial_{\xi}\xi_{\rm cd,0}\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)-\int^{\eta}_{0}\partial_{\xi}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\tau,\xi_{\rm cd,0}),\tau\big)d\tau,\end{split}

(4.145)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi,\eta)&=\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)-\int^{\eta}_{0}\partial_{\tau}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\tau,\xi_{\rm cd,0}),\tau\big)d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&=\partial_{\eta}\xi_{\rm cd,0}\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)-\int^{\eta}_{0}\partial_{\tau}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\tau,\xi_{\rm cd,0}),\tau\big)d\tau.\end{split}

(4.146)

For the terms
\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)
, we employ the boundary condition
\eqref{eq:4.97}_{2}
on
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}
again to deduce that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\quad\ \partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)\\[5.0pt]
&=\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)-2\Big(\partial_{\xi}\xi_{\rm cd,0}\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}+\partial_{\eta}\xi_{\rm cd,0}\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\Big)\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)\\[5.0pt]
&\quad-2\Big(\partial_{\xi}\xi_{\rm cd,0}\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}+\partial_{\eta}\xi_{\rm cd,0}\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\Big)\partial^{2}_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}(\xi_{\rm cd,0},0)-2c^{\prime}_{\rm cd}(\xi_{\rm cd,0}).\end{split}

(4.147)

Thus, by Lemma
4.6
and (
4.147
), and by letting
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small, there exist positive constants
\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{I,8}
and
\epsilon^{\prime}_{I,8}

depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
, such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{I,8}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime}_{I,8})
, it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}&\leq\|D\xi_{\rm cd,0}\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}\|\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}+\|D\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}+\|D\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ +\mathcal{C}\Big(\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)^{\prime}}_{0}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)^{\prime}}_{0}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\Big),\end{split}

(4.148)

where
\mathcal{C}=\mathcal{C}(\tilde{\underline{U}},L)>0
.

By the estimates (
4.143
) and (
4.148
), following the proof of Proposition
4.3
in
[
36
]
,
we can choose positive constants
\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{I,8}
and
\epsilon^{\prime}_{I,8}
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
, such that
for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{I,8}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime}_{I,8})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small,
it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}+\|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\bigg(\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\|_{{2,0;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}}+\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\bigg).\end{split}

(4.149)

Then we only need to estimate
[D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}]_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}\cap(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI})}
.
As in the proof of Proposition
4.4
, we take two points
\check{P}=(\xi_{\check{P}},\eta_{\check{P}}),\ \check{Q}=(\xi_{\check{Q}},\eta_{\check{Q}})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}
, satisfying (
4.105
) with
\check{P}_{-}(0)=(\xi_{\check{P}_{-}(0)},0)

and
\check{Q}_{-}(0)=(\xi_{\check{Q}_{-}(0)},0)
.
Next, we substitute them into (
4.145
) and (
4.146
) to obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P})&=\partial_{\xi}\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{P})\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P}_{-}(0))-\int^{\eta_{\check{P}}}_{0}\partial_{\xi}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big(\check{P}_{-}(\tau)\big)d\tau,\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P})&=\partial_{\eta}\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{P}_{-}(0))\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P}_{-}(0))-\int^{\eta_{\check{P}}}_{0}\partial_{\tau}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P}_{-}(\tau))d\tau,\end{split}

(4.150)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q})&=\partial_{\xi}\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{Q})\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}_{-}(0))-\int^{\eta_{\check{Q}}}_{0}\partial_{\xi}\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big(\check{Q}_{-}(\tau)\big)d\tau,\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{\eta}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q})&=\partial_{\eta}\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{Q}_{-}(0))\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}_{-}(0))-\int^{\eta_{\check{Q}}}_{0}\partial_{\tau}\big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}_{-}(\tau))d\tau.\end{split}

(4.151)

Without loss of generality, we also assume that
\eta_{\check{P}}<\eta_{\check{Q}}

and let
\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}})=(\chi^{(\rm n)}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}},\xi_{\check{Q}_{-}(0)}),\eta_{\check{P}})
.
By (
4.150
) and (
4.148
) on the contact discontinuity
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{P},\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}})\big)\Big|D\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{P})\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P}_{-}(0))-D\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}}))\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}_{-}(0))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
\leq&d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{P},\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}})\big)\Big|D\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{P})-D\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}})\Big|\big|\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P}_{-}(0))\big|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ +\mathcal{C}\big|D\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{Q}_{-}(0))\big|d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{P}_{-}(0),\check{Q}_{-}(0)\big)\Big|\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P}_{-}(0))-\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}_{-}(0))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\mathcal{C}\big[D\xi_{\rm cd,0}\big]_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}\big\|\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ +\mathcal{C}\big\|D\xi_{\rm cd,0}\big\|_{0,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{P}_{-}(0),\check{Q}_{-}(0)\big)\Big|\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P}_{-}(0))-\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}_{-}(0))\Big|.\end{split}

For the last term, by (
4.147
), we can choose positive constants
\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9}
and
\epsilon^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9}
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
, such that
for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small, it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{P}_{-}(0),\check{Q}_{-}(0)\big)\Big|\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P}_{-}(0))-\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}_{-}(0))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\mathcal{C}\bigg(\big\|D^{2}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{{0,\alpha;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}}+\big\|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\ +\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|(\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0})^{\prime}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|(\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0})^{\prime}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\bigg),\end{split}

where the constant
\mathcal{C}>0
depends only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
.

From the estimate (
4.149
) and by Lemma
4.6
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{P},\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}})\big)\Big|D\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{P})\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P}_{-}(0))-D\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}}))\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}_{-}(0))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\mathcal{C}\bigg(\big\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{{2,\alpha;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}}+\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\bigg),\end{split}

where the positive constant
\mathcal{C}
depends only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
.

Similarly to the arguments in the proof of Proposition
4.4
, we can also choose positive constants
\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9}
and
\epsilon^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9}

depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
, such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small, the following

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{\check{P},\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{P},\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}})\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P})-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ \ \leq\mathcal{C}\bigg(\big\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{{2,\alpha;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}}+\big\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\quad+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\bigg),\end{split}

holds by the Gronwall inequality.

On the other hand, by the equations (
4.145
)–(
4.146
) and (
4.151
), and the estimates (
4.97
)-(
4.98
) and (
4.149
), we can also deduce that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}}),\check{Q}\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}}),\check{Q}\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}}))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q})\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \leq\sup_{\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}}),\check{Q}\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}}),\check{Q}\big)\Big|D\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}}))-D\xi_{\rm cd,0}(\check{Q})\Big||\partial_{\xi_{\rm cd,0}}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P}_{-}(0))|\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ +\sup_{\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}}),\check{Q}\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{Q}_{-}(\eta_{\check{P}}),\check{Q}\big)\int^{\eta_{\check{Q}}}_{\eta_{\check{P}}}\bigg|D\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}}\Big)\partial_{\xi}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}_{-}(\tau))\bigg|d\tau\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \ \leq\mathcal{C}\bigg(\big\|D^{2}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{{0,0;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\big(\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0},\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big)\big\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\bigg),\end{split}

provided that
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small.
Here, the constant
\mathcal{C}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
.

With the two estimates above, we thus obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{\hat{P},\hat{Q}\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{P},\check{Q}\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P})-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q})\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \leq\sup_{\check{P}(\eta_{\check{P}}),\check{Q}(\eta_{\check{P}})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{P}(\eta_{\check{P}}),\check{Q}(\eta_{\check{P}})\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{P}(\eta_{\check{P}}))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}(\eta_{\check{P}}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ \ \ \ +\sup_{\check{Q}(\eta_{\check{P}}),\check{Q}(\eta_{\check{Q}})\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}d^{-\alpha}\big(\check{Q}(\eta_{\check{P}}),\check{Q}(\eta_{\check{Q}})\big)\Big|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}(\eta_{\check{P}}))-D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\check{Q}(\eta_{\check{Q}}))\Big|\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \leq\mathcal{C}\bigg(\big\|D^{2}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{{0,\alpha;(\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cup\{\mathcal{O}\})\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}}+\big\|D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\bigg),\end{split}

which gives the estimates on
\big[D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big]_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}
.

Finally, combining the estimates on
\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\|_{1,0;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}

and

\big[D\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big]_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{II}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{IV}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{VI}}
, there exist constants
\tilde{C}^{*}_{46}>0
, and

\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,9}=\max\{\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{\rm h,9},\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9},\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9}\}
and
\epsilon_{\rm h,9}=\min\{\epsilon^{\prime}_{\rm h,9},\epsilon^{\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9},\epsilon^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\rm h,9}\}

depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
, such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,9}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{\rm h,9})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
is sufficiently small,
the estimate (
4.141
) holds. ∎

With Propositions
4.3
-
4.5
, we now give the proof of Proposition
4.2
.

Proof of Proposition
4.2
.

We will show the estimate (
4.76
) by adopting the induction argument.
It follows from (
4.110
), (
4.120
), (
4.123
) and
(
4.140
) that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\delta z^{(n)}_{-}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}_{I}\cup\Omega_{II}\cup\Omega_{III}\cup\Omega_{IV}}\leq C^{*}_{1,-}\Big(\|\delta z_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}+\|g_{-}-1\|_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{-,0}\}}\Big),\end{aligned}

(4.152)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\delta z^{(n)}_{+}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}_{I}\cup\Omega_{II}\cup\Omega_{III}\cup\Omega_{IV}}\leq&C^{*}_{1,+}\Big(\|\delta z_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,h}\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ +\sum_{k=\rm e,h}\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\|_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0}\}}\Big).\end{aligned}

(4.153)

Let
\xi_{0}^{*}=\min\{\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,0},\,\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},0}\}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{0}^{(\rm h)}:=\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{0}\}
. Then, there exist constants
C^{*}_{1}>0
and

\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,0}=\max\{\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,7},\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,8},\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,9}\}
and
\epsilon_{\rm h,0}=\min\{\epsilon_{\rm h,7},\epsilon_{\rm h,8},\epsilon_{\rm h,9}\}

depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
, such that for
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,0}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon_{\rm h,0})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
is sufficiently small,
it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\delta z^{(\rm n)}\|_{1,\alpha;\Omega^{(\rm h)}_{0}}&\leq C^{*}_{1}\Big(\|\delta z_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,h}\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,h}\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \ \ \ \qquad+\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\|_{2,\alpha;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{0}\}}+\|g_{-}-1\|_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{0}\}}\Big).\end{aligned}

Let
(\xi^{*}_{\rm cd,1},0)
be the intersection point of the characteristics with the speed
\lambda_{+}
starting from

(\xi_{0}^{*},-m^{(\rm h)})
and the transonic contact discontinuity
\eta=0
.
Let
(\xi^{*}_{-m^{(\rm h)},1},-m^{(h)})
stand for the point where the characteristics with the speed
\lambda_{-}
starting from
(\xi_{0}^{*},0)
and the lower nozzle wall
\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{-}
intersect.
Set
\tilde{\Omega}_{1}^{(\rm h)}:=\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}\cap\{\xi_{0}^{*}\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{1}\}
.
Regarding the line
\xi=\xi_{0}^{*}
as
\xi=0
and then repeating the arguments for proving Propositions
4.3
–
4.5
again, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\delta z^{(\rm n)}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}_{1}^{(\rm h)}}&\leq C^{*}_{2}\Big(\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}\|_{1,\alpha;\{\xi=\xi^{*}_{1}\}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,h}\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,h}\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \ \ +\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\|_{{2,\alpha;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{\xi^{*}_{0}\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{1}\}}}+\|g_{-}-1\|_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{\xi^{*}_{0}\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{1}\}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
