&\leq\tilde{C}^{*}_{2}\Big(\|\delta z_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,h}\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,h}\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \ \ +\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\|_{{2,\alpha;\overline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{1}\}}}+\|g_{-}-1\|_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}\cap\{0\leq\xi\leq\xi^{*}_{1}\}}\Big).\end{aligned}

(4.154)

Finally, by (
4.91
), we notice that
\xi_{0}^{*}
depends on
\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{\pm}
and
m^{(\rm h)}
. Furthermore, (
4.98
) and the uniform bound of
m^{(\rm h)}

imply that
\xi_{0}^{*}
also has a uniform lower bound independent of
\rm n
and
\epsilon_{I}
. Thus,
we can repeat the process
\ell
times with
\ell=[\frac{L}{\xi^{*}_{0}}]+1
for the finite length
L
, where
\ell
has a uniform upper bound.

Define
\xi_{\ell}^{*}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\ell}^{(\rm h)}
.
Obviously
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}=\cup_{0\leq k\leq\ell}\tilde{\Omega}_{k}^{(\rm h)}
.
By taking the summation of all the estimates as in (
4.154
) altogether for
k=0
,
\cdot\cdot\cdot
,
\ell
,
we obtain (
4.76
). This completes the proof of Proposition
4.2
.
∎

Now we give the proof of the main result in this section.

Proof of Theorem
4.1
.

By the estimate (
4.9
) and Proposition
4.2
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}&\leq\mathcal{C}\bigg(\big\|\delta z_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}+{\big\|\delta\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,h}\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad+\sum_{k=\rm e,h}\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\big\|g_{-}+1\big\|_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}}+\big\|g_{+}-1\big\|_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{+}}\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad+\big\|\tilde{\omega}_{\rm e}\big\|^{(-1-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e},\mathcal{Q}_{\rm e}\})}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex}}+{\big\|\omega_{\rm cd}\big\|^{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}}\bigg).\end{split}

(4.155)

The we can choose constants
\alpha_{0}\in(0,1)
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
, and
\varrho_{0}>0
depending on
\tilde{\epsilon}
,
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
and let
\epsilon^{*}_{I}=\min\{\varepsilon_{\rm e,0},\epsilon^{*}_{\rm h,0}\}
,
\mathcal{C}^{*}_{0}=\max\{\mathcal{C}_{\rm e,0},\mathcal{C}_{\rm h,0}\}
,
such that for any
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{0})
,
\varrho\in(0,\varrho_{0})
and
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{*}_{0}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{*}_{I})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small, if
(\delta\varphi^{(\rm n-1)},\delta z^{(\rm n-1)})\in\mathscr{K}_{2\epsilon_{I}}
and
\omega_{\rm cd}\in\mathcal{W}
with
\|\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\leq\varrho
, we can get the estimate (
4.1
) by Proposition
4.1
and (
4.155
). This completes the proof of the Theorem
4.1
.

∎

5.
Determination of the Flow Slope Function
\omega_{\rm cd}
as a Fixed Point

In this section, we employ the implicit function theorem to show that the map
\mathbf{T}_{\omega}
admits a unique fixed point

\omega_{\rm cd}
.
For the precise statement of the implicit function theorem, we refer to Theorem 4.B on page 150 of the book
[
54
]
.

For a fixed
\rm n
, we define

\displaystyle\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd};\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0})=\mathbf{T}_{\omega}(\omega_{\rm cd};\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0})-\omega_{\rm cd}=\tan\Big(\frac{\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}+\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}}{2}\Big)-\omega_{\rm cd},

(5.1)

where
\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0}=(\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}_{0},\tilde{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0},\tilde{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0},z_{0},\tilde{B}^{(\rm h)}_{0},\tilde{S}^{(\rm h)}_{0},\tilde{\omega}_{\rm e},g_{+},g_{-})
.
Let

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{W}_{0}&=C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in})\times C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in})\times C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in})\times(C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}))^{2}\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\times C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in})\times C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in})\times C^{2,\alpha}_{(-1-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e},\mathcal{Q}_{\rm e}\})}(\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex})\times C^{2,\alpha}(\tilde{\Gamma}_{+})\times C^{2,\alpha}(\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}).\end{split}

(5.2)

Then the map

\displaystyle\mathscr{F}:\ {\mathcal{W}}\times\mathcal{W}_{0}\longrightarrow{\mathcal{W}}\ \ \ \ \mbox{with}\ \ \ \mathscr{F}(0,\underline{\boldsymbol{\omega}})=0,

(5.3)

with
\underline{\boldsymbol{\omega}}=(\underline{p}^{(\rm e)},\underline{\tilde{B}}^{(\rm e)},\underline{\tilde{S}}^{(\rm e)},\underline{z},\underline{\tilde{B}}^{(\rm h)},\underline{\tilde{S}}^{(\rm h)},0,1,-1)
has the property
in the following proposition.

Proposition 5.1
.

For any given
m^{(\rm e)}\in\mathcal{M}_{\sigma}
with
\sigma>0
sufficiently small, there exists a constant
\tilde{\epsilon}^{*}_{0}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
,
such that if

\displaystyle\underline{M}^{(\rm h)}\geq\sqrt{1+L^{2}/4},

(5.4)

with
\underline{M}^{(\rm h)}=\frac{\underline{u}^{(\rm h)}}{\underline{c}^{(\rm h)}}

and

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0}-\underline{\boldsymbol{\omega}}\|_{\mathcal{W}_{0}}<\tilde{\epsilon},

(5.5)

with
\tilde{\epsilon}\in(0,\tilde{\epsilon}^{*}_{0})
, the equation
\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd};\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0})=0

admits a unique solution
\omega_{\rm cd}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}C^{1,\alpha}_{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}(\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd})}
satisfying

\displaystyle{\|\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{(-\alpha,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\}})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}}\leq C_{\omega}\tilde{\epsilon},

(5.6)

where
C_{\omega}>0
is a constant depending only on
\underline{\boldsymbol{\omega}}
and
L
.

Proof.

We will divide the proof into three steps. For the simplicity of notation,
we write
\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd};\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0})

and its derivatives
\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd};\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0}]
with respect to
\omega_{\rm cd}
as
\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd})

and
\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}]
, respectively.

Step 1
: Definition of the map
\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}]
.

For any
\omega_{\rm cd}
,
\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\in\mathcal{W}
satisfying the Theorem
4.1
, and for
\tau>0
,
we consider the limit of

\displaystyle\frac{\big\|\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd})-\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd})-\tau\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}](\delta\omega_{\rm cd})\big\|^{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}}{\tau\big\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\big\|^{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}}

as
\tau\rightarrow 0
.

Let
(\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}},\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}})
and
(\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}},\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}})
be the solutions of the problem
(\widetilde{\mathbf{FP}})_{\rm n}
with the boundary conditions:

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}=\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd},\ \ \ \ \partial_{\xi}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=\omega_{\rm cd},

on
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}
, respectively.
Denote

\displaystyle\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=\frac{\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}}{\tau},\quad\ \ \dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=\frac{\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}}{\tau}.

By the direct computation, we know that
(\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}},\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}})
satisfies the following boundary value problem:

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\sum_{i,j=1,2}\partial_{i}\big(a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}\partial_{j}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big)=\sum_{i,j=1,2}\partial_{i}\Big(\frac{a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}-a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}}{\tau}\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}\Big),&\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{2}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}\partial_{2}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\eta),&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\xi),&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{+},\\[5.0pt]
\delta\varphi^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=\int^{\xi}_{0}\delta\omega_{\rm cd}(\nu)d\nu,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}=2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\partial_{1}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}+2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{2}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}},&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{-},\end{array}\right.

where
\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{\pm}
,
\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{0,i}
and
\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\textrm{cd},i}(i=1,2)
are defined as
in (
3.102
)-(
3.103
) and (
3.107
),

\displaystyle a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,k}=\int^{1}_{0}\partial_{\partial_{j}\varphi}\mathcal{N}_{i}\big(D\underline{\varphi}+\varsigma D\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{k};\tilde{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0},\tilde{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0}\big)d\varsigma,\ \ (i,j=1,2),\ \ k=\omega_{\rm cd},\ \omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd},

satisfying (
4.20
)-(
4.23
),

\displaystyle\begin{split}g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\eta)&=-\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\eta}_{0}\bigg(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-\underline{\beta}_{0,j}\bigg)\partial_{j}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}d\mu\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ -\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\eta}_{0}\bigg(\frac{\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}}}{\tau}\bigg)\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}d\mu,\end{split}

and
g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\xi)=g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}(m^{(\rm e)})
, with

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}=\int^{1}_{0}\partial_{\partial_{j}\varphi}\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}\big(D\underline{\varphi}+\nu D\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}};\tilde{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0},\tilde{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0}\big)d\nu,\\[5.0pt]
&\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}}=\int^{1}_{0}\partial_{\partial_{j}\varphi}\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}\big(D\underline{\varphi}+\nu D\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}};\tilde{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0},\tilde{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0}\big)d\nu.\end{split}

(5.18)

As a special case of Section 4, the problem (
5
) has a unique solution
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\in{C^{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}_{2,\alpha}}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})
and
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\in C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)})

satisfying

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\quad\ \|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\|\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|\omega_{0}-\underline{\omega}\|_{\mathcal{W}_{0}}+\|\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}+\tau\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\Big)\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\ +\mathcal{C}\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\tilde{\epsilon}+\varrho+\tau\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\Big)\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\mathcal{C}\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}},\end{split}

(5.19)

for

with

depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
, where we have used the estimate (

) in Theorem

for
\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}
by changing the boundary data

\omega_{\rm cd}
to

First, we choose

,

and

depending only on

,

and
\alpha
such that

,

and
\mathcal{C}\tau_{0}\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\leq\frac{1}{4}
.
Then, for
\tilde{\epsilon}\in(0,\tilde{\epsilon}^{*}_{0})
,
\varrho\in(0,\varrho^{*}_{0})
and
\tau\in(0,\tau_{0})
, we can get by (
5.19
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\|\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\leq\mathcal{C}\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}},\end{split}

(5.20)

where the constant
\mathcal{C}>0
depends only on

,
L
and
\alpha
.

Therefore, by the compactness, we can
let
(\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}},\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}})
be the limit of the solutions

(\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}},\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}})
as
\tau\rightarrow 0
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\sum_{i,j=1,2}\partial_{i}\big(a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}\partial_{j}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big)=\sum_{i,j=1,2}\partial_{i}\Big(Da^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}\cdot D\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\Big),&\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{2}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}\partial_{2}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=g^{(\rm n,0)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\eta),&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=g^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\xi),&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{+},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=\int^{\xi}_{0}\delta\omega_{\rm cd}(\nu)d\nu,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}=2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\partial_{1}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}+2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{2}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}},&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{-},\end{array}\right.

with

\displaystyle\begin{split}g^{(\rm n,0)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\eta)&=-\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\eta}_{0}\bigg(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}}-\underline{\beta}_{0,j}\bigg)\partial_{j}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}d\mu\\[5.0pt]

(5.32)

Moreover, by the methods used in Section 4, we have the following estimate

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\|\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\leq\mathcal{C}\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{(-\alpha;\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}},\end{split}

and

Now, let us consider the convergence rate of the limits
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}

and
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}

. By (

),

and

satisfy the following problem:

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\sum_{i,j=1,2}\partial_{i}\big(a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}\partial_{j}(\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}})\big)\\[5.0pt]
\ =\sum_{i,j=1,2}\partial_{i}\bigg(\frac{a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}-a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}}{\tau}\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-Da^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}\cdot D\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\bigg),&\ \mbox{in}\ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}(\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}})+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{2}(\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}})=0,&\ \mbox{in}\ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}(\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}})+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}\partial_{2}(\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}})=0,&\ \mbox{in}\ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]

\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \mbox{on}\ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\xi)-g^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\xi),&\ \mbox{on}\ \tilde{\Gamma}_{+},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \mbox{on}\ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]

\qquad\ \ =2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\partial_{1}(\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}})+2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{2}(\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}),&\ \mbox{on}\ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \mbox{on}\ \tilde{\Gamma}_{-},\end{array}\right.

with
g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\xi)-g^{(\rm n,0)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\xi)=g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}(m^{(\rm e)})-g^{(\rm n,0)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}(m^{(\rm e)})
and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\eta)-g^{(\rm n,0)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}(\eta)\\[5.0pt]
&=-\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\eta}_{0}\bigg(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-\underline{\beta}_{0,j}\bigg)\Big(\partial_{j}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\partial_{j}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\Big)d\mu\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ -\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\eta}_{0}\bigg(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}}\bigg)\partial_{j}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}d\mu\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ -\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\eta}_{0}\bigg(\frac{\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}}}{\tau}-D\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}}D\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\bigg)\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}d\mu,\end{split}

(5.47)

satisfying

\displaystyle\big\|g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}-g^{(\rm n,0)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(-1-\alpha,\{\mathcal{O},\mathcal{Q}_{\rm I}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\Bigg\|\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\eta}_{0}\bigg(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-\underline{\beta}_{0,j}\bigg)\Big(\partial_{j}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\partial_{j}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\Big)d\mu\Bigg\|^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(-1-\alpha,\{\mathcal{O},\mathcal{Q}_{\rm I}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\Bigg\|\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\eta}_{0}\bigg(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}}\bigg)\partial_{j}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}d\mu\Bigg\|^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(-1-\alpha,\{\mathcal{O},\mathcal{Q}_{\rm I}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\Bigg\|\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\eta}_{0}\bigg(\frac{\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}}}{\tau}-D\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j,\omega_{\rm cd}}D\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\bigg)\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}d\mu\Bigg\|^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(-1-\alpha,\{\mathcal{O},\mathcal{Q}_{\rm I}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{C}\big\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\tau

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\bigg(\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\bigg)^{2}\big\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\tau,

for

In the same way, by choosing

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}-g^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(-1-\alpha,\{\mathcal{Q}_{\rm I},\mathcal{Q}_{\rm e}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{+}}\\[5.0pt]
