&\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\tau\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\bigg(\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\bigg)^{2}\big\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\tau.\end{split}

(5.48)

Similarly to the proof of Proposition
4.1
in Section 4.1, we have in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}
that

\displaystyle\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}\Bigg(\sum_{i,j=1,2}\bigg\|\frac{a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}-a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}}{\tau}\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-Da^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}\cdot D\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\bigg\|^{{(-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\Bigg(\Big\|g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}-g^{(\rm n,0)}_{0,\omega_{\rm cd}}\Big\|^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(-1-\alpha,\{\mathcal{O},\mathcal{Q}_{\rm I}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}+\Big\|g^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}-g^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}\Big\|^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(-1-\alpha,\{\mathcal{Q}_{\rm I},\mathcal{Q}_{\rm e}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{+}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}\sum_{i,j=1,2}\bigg\|Da^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}\cdot D\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\Big(\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}-\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\Big)\bigg\|^{{(-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{C}\sum_{i,j=1,2}\bigg\|\Big(\frac{a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}-a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}}{\tau}-Da^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega_{\rm cd}}\cdot D\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\Big)\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}\bigg\|^{{(-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\big\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\tau

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\bigg(\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\bigg)^{2}\big\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|^{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|^{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\tau+\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\Big)^{2}\tau

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\big\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\tau

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\big\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\bigg(\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\bigg)^{2}\tau,

where the constant
\mathcal{C}=\mathcal{C}(\underline{\tilde{U}},L,\alpha)>0
is independent of
\rm n
. Proposition
4.1
and (
5.5
) yield

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\quad\ \big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|\omega_{0}-\underline{\omega}\|_{\mathcal{W}_{0}}+\|\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}+\tau\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\Big)\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ +\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\big\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\Big)^{2}\tau\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\tilde{\epsilon}+\varrho+\tau\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\Big)\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\big\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\Big)^{2}\tau.\end{split}

Thus we can choose
\tilde{\epsilon}^{*}_{0}>0
,
\varrho^{*}_{0}>0
and
\tau_{0}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
such that
\mathcal{C}\epsilon^{*}_{0}\leq\frac{1}{4}
,
\mathcal{C}\varrho^{*}_{0}\leq\frac{1}{4}
and
\mathcal{C}\tau_{0}\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\leq\frac{1}{4}
, and then for
\tilde{\epsilon}\in(0,\tilde{\epsilon}^{*}_{0})
,
\varrho\in(0,\varrho^{*}_{0})
and
\tau\in(0,\tau_{0})
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\big\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\Big)^{2}\tau,\end{split}

(5.49)

where
\mathcal{C}>0
is a constant depending only on
\underline{\tilde{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
.

With the estimate for
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}
and together
with the boundary condition on
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}
, we can further estimate the solution
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}

as in Section 4.2 to deduce that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\leq\mathcal{C}\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}.\end{split}

(5.50)

Hence, it follows from (
5.49
) and (
5.50
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\big\|\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\big\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\Big)^{2}\tau.\end{split}

(5.51)

Therefore,
(\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}},\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}})\rightarrow(\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}},\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}})
as
\tau\rightarrow 0
in

C^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)})
.

Define

\displaystyle\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}](\delta\omega_{\rm cd}):=\frac{1}{2}\sec^{2}\Big(\frac{\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}}{2}\Big)\big(\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}\big)-\delta\omega_{\rm cd},

(5.52)

which is a linear map.
Then, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd})-\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd})-\tau\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}](\delta\omega_{\rm cd})\\[5.0pt]
&\quad=\tan\Big(\frac{\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}+\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}}}{2}\Big)-\tan\Big(\frac{\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}}{2}\Big)-\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \ \quad-\frac{1}{2}\tau\sec^{2}\Big(\frac{\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}}{2}\Big)\big(\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}\big)+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\\[5.0pt]
&\quad=\frac{\tau}{2}\int^{1}_{0}\sec^{2}\Big(\frac{\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\varsigma\tau(\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}})}{2}\Big)d\varsigma\Big(\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ \ \quad-\frac{\tau}{2}\sec^{2}\Big(\frac{\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}}{2}\Big)\big(\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}\big).\end{split}

Thus, by (
5.20
) and (
5.51
), we can further deduce that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd})-\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd})-\tau\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}](\delta\omega_{\rm cd})\big\|^{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\tau\|\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|^{2}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\big\|\dot{z}^{(\rm n,\tau)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\Big)\tau\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\big\|^{{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\Big)^{2}\tau^{2},\end{split}

which implies that

\displaystyle\frac{\big\|\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd}+\tau\delta\omega_{\rm cd})-\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd})-\tau\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}](\delta\omega_{\rm cd})\big\|^{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}}{\tau\big\|\delta\omega_{\rm cd}\big\|^{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}}\rightarrow 0,

as
\tau\rightarrow 0
. Thus,
\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}]
is a Fréchet derivative with respect to
\omega_{\rm cd}
for the functional
\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd})
.

Step 2
: Continuity of the map
\mathscr{F}
and
\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}]

at the point
(\omega_{\rm cd},\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0})=(0,\underline{\boldsymbol{\omega}})
.

The continuity of map
\mathscr{F}
at the point
(\omega_{\rm cd},\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0})=(0,\underline{\boldsymbol{\omega}})

follows directly from Theorem
4.1
. Now, let us consider the continuity of the map

\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}]
.

For any fixed
\delta\omega_{\textrm{cd}}
with
\|\delta\omega_{\textrm{cd}}\|^{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}<+\infty
,

we assume
\omega^{k}_{\textrm{cd}}\rightarrow\omega_{\textrm{cd}}
in
{C^{1,\alpha}_{(-\alpha,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\}})}}(\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd})

as
k\rightarrow\infty
, and will show that as
k\rightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}](\delta\omega_{\textrm{c}d})\rightarrow\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}](\delta\omega_{\textrm{c}d}),\ \ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ {C^{1,\alpha}_{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}(\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd})}.

(5.53)

By (
5
), the solutions

(\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\textrm{c}d}},\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}})

corresponding to
\omega^{k}_{\textrm{c}d}
satisfy

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\sum_{i,j=1,2}\partial_{i}\big(a^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}\partial_{j}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}\big)=\sum_{i,j=1,2}\partial_{i}\Big(Da^{(\rm n)}_{ij,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}\cdot D\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}\Big),&\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\partial_{2}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}+\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}\partial_{2}\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}=g^{(\rm n,0)}_{0,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}},&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}=g^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}},&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{+},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}=\int^{\nu}_{0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta\omega_{\rm cd}(\nu)}d\nu,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}=2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\partial_{1}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}+2\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{2}\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}},&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}+\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}=0,&\ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{-},\end{array}\right.

where
g^{(\rm n,0)}_{0,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}
is given in (
5.32
) by changing
\omega_{\rm cd}
to
\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}

and
g^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}(\xi)=g^{(\rm n,0)}_{0,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}(m^{(\rm e)})
.

In the same way as in
Step 1
, we know that

\displaystyle(\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}},\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}})\rightarrow(\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}},\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}),\qquad\mbox{in}\qquad C^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}),

and

\displaystyle\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{\pm,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}\rightarrow\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{\pm,\omega_{\rm cd}},\qquad\mbox{in}\quad C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}),\quad\mbox{as}\quad k\rightarrow\infty.

Then

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\quad\ \big\|\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}](\delta\omega_{\textrm{c}d})-\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}](\delta\omega_{\textrm{c}d})\big\|^{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\frac{1}{2}\bigg\|\sec^{2}\Big(\frac{\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}+\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}}{2}\Big)\big(\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}+\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}\big)-\sec^{2}\Big(\frac{\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}}{2}\Big)\big(\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}\big)\bigg\|^{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}-\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\|\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega^{k}_{\rm cd}}-\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&\rightarrow 0,\qquad\mbox{as}\qquad k\rightarrow\infty,\end{split}

which implies that (
5.53
) holds by (
5.50
) and Theorem
4.1
.

Step 3
:
{\left.\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}]\right.}\big|_{(\omega_{\rm cd},\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0})=(0,\underline{\boldsymbol{\omega}})}
is an isomorphism.

We need to show that for any given function
f\in{C^{1,\alpha}_{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}(\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd})
,
there exists a unique
\widehat{\delta\omega}_{\rm cd}\in C^{1,\alpha}_{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}(\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd})
such that

\Big(\left.\mathscr{F}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}[\omega_{\rm cd}]\right.\big|_{(\omega_{\rm cd},\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0})=(0,\underline{\boldsymbol{\omega}})}\Big)(\widehat{\delta\omega}_{\rm cd})=f
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\Big(\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}\Big)-\widehat{\delta\omega}_{\rm cd}=f,\ \ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}.

(5.65)

At the background state, the solution
\widehat{\delta z^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}
satisfies

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}e_{1}\partial_{11}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}+e_{2}\partial_{22}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Omega}}^{(\rm e)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\underline{\lambda}_{+}\partial_{2}\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Omega}}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\underline{\lambda}_{-}\partial_{2}\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Omega}}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex},\\[5.0pt]
\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{+},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=\widehat{\delta\omega}_{\rm cd},&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}-\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}=2\underline{\beta}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{2}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}},&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}+\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{+,\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{-},\end{array}\right.

where
e_{1}
,
e_{2}
are given by (
4.20
) and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\underline{\lambda}_{+}=-\underline{\lambda}_{-}=\frac{\underline{\rho}^{(\rm h)}\underline{u}^{(\rm h)}\underline{c}^{(\rm h)}}{\sqrt{(\underline{u}^{(\rm h)})^{2}-(\underline{c}^{(\rm h)})^{2}}},\ \ \ \underline{\beta}_{\rm cd,2}=\frac{(\underline{\rho}^{(\rm e)}\underline{c}^{(\rm e)})^{2}(\underline{u}^{(\rm e)})^{3}\sqrt{(\underline{u}^{(\rm h)})^{2}-(\underline{c}^{(\rm h)})^{2}}}{\underline{\rho}^{(\rm h)}\underline{c}^{(\rm h)}(\underline{u}^{(\rm h)})^{2}\big((\underline{c}^{(\rm e)})^{2}-(\underline{u}^{(\rm e)})^{2}\big)}>0.\end{split}

From (
5.4
), since

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{m}^{(\rm h)}}{\underline{\lambda}_{+}}=\frac{\underline{\rho}^{(\rm h)}\underline{u}^{(\rm h)}}{\underline{\lambda}_{+}}=\sqrt{(\underline{M}^{(\rm h)})^{2}-1}\geq L/2,

by the characteristic method we know that
\widehat{\dot{z}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{-,\omega_{\rm cd}}=0

in
\underline{\tilde{\Omega}}^{(\rm h)}
. By (
5.65
), the
boundary condition for
\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}
on
\underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}
is

\displaystyle\partial_{1}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}+\underline{\beta}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{2}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=-f,\ \ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}.

(5.77)

Then solving
\widehat{\delta\omega}_{\rm cd}
is equivalent to solving the following boundary value problem for

\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}
in
\underline{\tilde{\Omega}}^{(\rm e)}
for a given function
f\in C^{1,\alpha}_{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}(\tilde{\underline{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd})
:

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}e_{1}\partial_{11}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}+e_{2}\partial_{22}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Omega}}^{(\rm e)},\\[5.0pt]
\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}+\underline{\beta}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{2}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=-f,&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}.\end{array}\right.

In order to show the existence and uniqueness of problem (
5
), we introduce a new function

\displaystyle\widehat{\delta\psi^{(\rm n)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}:=\partial_{1}\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}.

(5.83)

Then,
\widehat{\delta\psi^{(\rm n)}}
satisfies

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}e_{1}\partial_{11}\widehat{\delta\psi^{(\rm n)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}+e_{2}\partial_{22}\widehat{\delta\psi^{(\rm n)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Omega}}^{(\rm e)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\widehat{\delta\psi^{(\rm n)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\widehat{\delta\psi^{(\rm n)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=0,&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\widehat{\delta\psi^{(\rm n)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}+\underline{\beta}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{2}\widehat{\delta\psi^{(\rm n)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}=-\partial_{1}f,&\qquad\ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}_{\rm cd}.\end{array}\right.

By Theorem 1.1 in
[
39
]
, (
5
) admits a unique solution

\widehat{\delta\psi^{(\rm n)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\in C^{(-\alpha,{\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\}})}_{1,\alpha}(\underline{\tilde{\Omega}}^{(\rm e)})
.
Then, by (
5.83
) and the boundary condition on
\underline{\tilde{\Gamma}}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex}

for
\widehat{\delta\psi^{(\rm n)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}
, we see that

\displaystyle\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}(\xi,\eta)=\int^{\xi}_{L}\widehat{\delta\psi^{(\rm n)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}(s,\eta)ds.

By (
5
), it implies
\widehat{\dot{\varphi}^{(\rm n,0)}}_{\omega_{\rm cd}}\in C^{(-1-\alpha,{\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\}})}_{2,\alpha}(\underline{\tilde{\Omega}}^{(\rm e)})
.

Now, we can apply the implicit function theorem to conclude that, there exists a small constant
\tilde{\epsilon}^{*}_{0}>0
depending only on
\underline{U}
and
L
such that
for
\epsilon\in(0,\tilde{\epsilon}^{*}_{0})
, the equation
\mathscr{F}(\omega_{\rm cd},\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0})=0
admits a unique solution

\omega_{\rm cd}=\omega_{\rm cd}(\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0})
satisfying

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\|\omega_{\rm cd}\|^{(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}}\leq\tilde{C}_{\omega}\|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{0}-\underline{\boldsymbol{\omega}}\|_{\mathcal{W}_{0}}\leq\tilde{C}_{\omega}\tilde{\epsilon},\end{split}

where
\tilde{C}_{\omega}
depends only on
\underline{\boldsymbol{\omega}}
and
L
.

∎

6.
Existence and Uniqueness of Problem
(\mathbf{FP})

Based on the arguments in Sections 3-4, we will prove in this section that the map
\mathcal{J}
in (
3.110
) constructed by the iteration algorithm
(\mathbf{FP})_{\rm n}

is a well-defined contractive map so that there exists a fixed point, which is actually the solution of the fixed boundary value problem
(\mathbf{FP})
. We summarize this in the following lemma.

Lemma 6.1
.

For any given
m^{(\rm e)}\in\mathcal{M}_{\sigma}
with
\sigma>0
sufficiently small and under the assumptions (
3.123
)-(
3.124
) in Theorem
3.3
,
there exist some constants
\alpha_{0}\in(0,1)
,
\mathcal{C}^{*}_{I,0}>0
and
\tilde{\epsilon}^{*}_{I,0}>0
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
, such that for each
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{0})
and
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{*}_{I,0}\tilde{\epsilon},\tilde{\epsilon}^{*}_{I,0})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small,

the iteration algorithm
(\mathbf{FP})_{\rm n}
generates a well-defined sequence
\big\{(\varphi^{(\rm n)},z^{(\rm n)})\big\}^{\infty}_{n=1}
in
\mathscr{K}_{2{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon_{I}}}
.
Moreover, the sequence
\big\{(\varphi^{(\rm n)},z^{(\rm n)})\big\}^{\infty}_{\rm n=1}

is convergent in
C^{1,\alpha}\big(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}\big)\times C^{0,\alpha}\big(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}\big)

and its limit is the unique solution of the fixed boundary value problem
(\mathbf{FP})
.

Proof.

Firstly, it follows from Theorem
4.1
and Proposition
5.1
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\|\delta z^{(\rm n)}\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&C^{*}\bigg({\big\|\delta\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{B}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}+\sum_{k=\rm e,\rm h}\big\|\delta\tilde{S}^{(\rm k)}_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm k)}_{\rm in}}\\[5.0pt]
&\qquad+\big\|\delta z_{0}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}+{\big\|\omega_{\rm cd}\big\|^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e}\})}}_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}}+\big\|\tilde{\omega}_{\rm e}\big\|^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(-1-\alpha,\{\mathcal{P}_{\rm e},\mathcal{Q}_{\rm e}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}}+\big\|g_{+}-1\big\|_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{+}}+\big\|g_{-}+1\big\|_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{-}}\bigg)\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\tilde{C}^{*}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}},\end{split}

where the constant
\tilde{C}^{*}=\tilde{C}^{*}(\tilde{\underline{U}},L)>0
is independent of
\rm n
and
\tilde{\epsilon}
.
By taking
\tilde{\epsilon}
small enough we can get that
\tilde{C}^{*}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\leq 2{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon_{I}}
, which in turn
indicates that the iteration algorithm
(\mathbf{FP})_{\rm n}
is well-defined in

\mathscr{K}_{2{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon_{I}}}
. This means that the map
\mathcal{J}
given in (
3.108
) is well defined and maps
\mathscr{K}_{2{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon_{I}}}
to itself:

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}:\ \mathscr{K}_{2{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon_{I}}}\longmapsto\mathscr{K}_{2{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon_{I}}}.

Next, we will show that
\mathcal{J}
is a contraction mapping in
C^{1}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{0}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)})
which admits a fixed point in
C^{2,\alpha}_{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{1}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)})
.
Let

(\varphi^{(\rm n+1)},z^{(\rm n+1)}):=\mathcal{J}(\varphi^{(\rm n)},z^{(\rm n)})
and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}=\delta z^{(\rm n+1)}-\delta z^{(\rm n)},\ \ \ \delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}=\delta\varphi^{(\rm n+1)}-\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)},\end{split}

(6.1)

where
\delta z^{(\rm n+1)},\ \delta z^{(\rm n)}
and
\delta\varphi^{(\rm n+1)},\ \delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}

are the solutions to the
(\mathbf{FP})_{\rm n}
, respectively.
Then,
\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}
and
\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}
satisfy

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\sum_{i,j=1,2}\partial_{j}\Big(a^{(\rm n)}_{ij}\partial_{i}\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}\Big)=\sum_{i,j=1,2}\partial_{j}\Big(\big(a^{(\rm n)}_{ij}-a^{(\rm n+1)}_{ij}\big)\partial_{i}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n+1)}\Big),&\ \ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}+\textrm{diag}\big(\lambda^{(\rm n)}_{+},\ \lambda^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big)\partial_{2}\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}\\[5.0pt]
\ \ \qquad\qquad=-\textrm{diag}\Big(\lambda^{(\rm n)}_{+}-\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+},\ \lambda^{(\rm n)}_{-}-\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}\Big)\partial_{2}\delta z^{(\rm n)},&\ \ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}=g^{(\rm n+1)}_{0}(\eta)-g^{(\rm n)}_{0}(\eta),&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{1}\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}=0,&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex},\\[5.0pt]
\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}=0,&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}=g^{(\rm n+1)}_{+}(\xi)-g^{(\rm n)}_{+}(\xi),&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{+},\\[5.0pt]
\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{-}+\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{+}=2\arctan\partial_{1}(\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}+\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)})-2\arctan\partial_{1}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)},&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{-}-\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{+}=2\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,1}\partial_{1}\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}+2\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{2}\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}\\[5.0pt]
\ \ \ \qquad\qquad+2\big(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,1}-\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\big)\partial_{1}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}+2\big(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,2}-\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\big)\partial_{2}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)},&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{-}+\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{+}=0,&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{-},\end{array}\right.

where
a^{(\ell)}_{ij}=\int^{1}_{0}\partial_{i}\mathcal{N}_{i}(D\underline{\varphi}+\varsigma D\delta\varphi^{(\ell)})d\varsigma

for
\ell=\rm n,\ n+1
, and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&g^{(\rm n+1)}_{0}-g^{(\rm n)}_{0}=-\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\eta}_{0}\bigg(\Big(\beta^{(\rm n+1)}_{0,j}-\underline{\beta}_{0,j}\Big)\partial_{j}\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}-\Big(\beta^{(\rm n+1)}_{0,j}-\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j}\Big)\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\bigg)d\mu,\\[5.0pt]
&g^{(\rm n+1)}_{+}-g^{(\rm n)}_{+}=-\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{m^{(\rm e)}}_{0}\bigg(\Big(\beta^{(\rm n+1)}_{0,j}-\underline{\beta}_{0,j}\Big)\partial_{j}\delta\phi^{(\rm n+1)}-\Big(\beta^{(\rm n+1)}_{0,j}-\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j}\Big)\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\bigg)d\mu.\end{split}

(6.14)

Here
\beta^{(\rm n+1)}_{0,j},\ \beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j},j=1,2
are defined by (
3.107
) for
\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}
and
\delta\varphi^{(\rm n+1)}
.

Choosing
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small with
|\lambda_{\pm}|/m^{(\rm h)}\geq L/2
and then
following the argument in Section 4.2, we know that there exists a constant
C>0

depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{-}\big\|_{0,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}&\leq C\bigg(\big\|\big(\lambda^{(\rm n)}_{+}-\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{+}\big)\partial_{2}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{-}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\big\|\big(\lambda^{(\rm n)}_{-}-\lambda^{(\rm n-1)}_{-}\big)\partial_{2}\delta z^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\bigg)\\[5.0pt]
&\leq C\epsilon_{I}\big\|\delta Z^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{0,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}.\end{split}

(6.15)

On the other hand, eliminating
\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{+}
on
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}
gives the boundary condition:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\tilde{\beta}^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,1}\partial_{1}\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}+\tilde{\beta}^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{2}\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}\\[5.0pt]
=&\big(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,1}-\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\big)\partial_{1}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}+\big(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,2}-\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\big)\partial_{2}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}+\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{-},&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\end{split}

(6.16)

where

\displaystyle\begin{split}\tilde{\beta}^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,1}=\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,1}+\int^{1}_{0}\frac{d\varsigma}{1+(\partial_{1}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}+\varsigma\partial_{1}\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)})^{2}},\qquad\ \ \tilde{\beta}^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,2}=\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,2}.\end{split}

For the solution
\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}
in
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}
with boundary condition (
6.16
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}\big\|_{1,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&C\Big\|\sum_{i,j=1,2}\partial_{j}\Big(\big(a^{(\rm n)}_{ij}-a^{(\rm n+1)}_{ij}\big)\partial_{i}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n+1)}\Big)\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\big\|g^{(\rm n+1)}_{0}-g^{(\rm n)}_{0}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}+\big\|g^{(\rm n+1)}_{+}-g^{(\rm n)}_{+}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{+}}\\[5.0pt]
&+C\Big\|\big(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,1}-\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\big)\partial_{1}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}+\big(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,2}-\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\big)\partial_{2}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}+\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{-}\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&C\sum_{j=1,2}\bigg\|\int^{\eta}_{0}\bigg(\Big(\beta^{(\rm n+1)}_{0,j}-\underline{\beta}_{0,j}\Big)\partial_{j}\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}-\Big(\beta^{(\rm n+1)}_{0,j}-\beta^{(\rm n)}_{0,j}\Big)\partial_{j}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\bigg)d\mu\bigg\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ +C\Big\|\big(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,1}-\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\big)\partial_{1}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}+\big(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,2}-\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\big)\partial_{2}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}+\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{-}\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ +C\big\|\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\big\|\delta\Phi^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{1,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\big\|\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{-}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&C{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(\tilde{\epsilon}+\epsilon_{I}\big)}\Big(\big\|\delta Z^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{0,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\big\|\delta\Phi^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{1,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\Big)+C{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon_{I}}\big\|\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}\big\|_{1,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}},\end{split}

(6.17)

where we have used the estimate (
6.15
), provided that
\sigma>0
is sufficiently small.
Then, similarly to Section 4.2, we can solve
\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{+}
with the boundary condition on
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}
and
the initial data on
\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}
, together with the estimates (
6.15
) and (
6.17
). Thus,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{+}\big\|_{0,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&C\Big\|2\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,1}\partial_{1}\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}+2\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,2}\partial_{2}\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}+C\Big\|2\big(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,1}-\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,1}\big)\partial_{1}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\\[5.0pt]
&\quad+C\Big\|2\big(\beta^{(\rm n)}_{\rm cd,2}-\beta^{(\rm n-1)}_{\rm cd,2}\big)\partial_{2}\delta\varphi^{(\rm n)}\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}+\big\|\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}_{-}\big\|_{0,\alpha,\tilde{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&C{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(\tilde{\epsilon}+\epsilon_{I}\big)}\Big(\big\|\delta Z^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{0,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\big\|\delta\Phi^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{1,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\Big)+C{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon_{I}}\big\|\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}\big\|_{1,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}.\end{split}

(6.18)

Finally, combining the estimates (
6.15
), (
6.17
) and (
6.18
) together, we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\big\|\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}\big\|_{0,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\\[5.0pt]
&\quad\leq C{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(\tilde{\epsilon}+\epsilon_{I}\big)}\Big(\big\|\delta Z^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{0,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\big\|\delta\Phi^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{1,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\Big)+C{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon_{I}}\big\|\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}\big\|_{1,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}},\end{split}

where constant
C>0
depends only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
.

Then we can choose constants
\mathcal{C}^{*}_{I,0}>0
,
\epsilon^{*}_{I,0}>0
and
\alpha_{0}\in(0,1)
depending on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
, such that for any
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{0})

and
\tilde{\epsilon}<\epsilon_{I}\in(\mathcal{C}^{*}_{I,0}\tilde{\epsilon},\epsilon^{*}_{I})
with
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small, the following holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\big\|\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)}\big\|_{0,\alpha,\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\Big(\big\|\delta\Phi^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\big\|\delta Z^{(\rm n)}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\Big),\end{split}

(6.19)

i.e.,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\mathcal{J}(\delta\Phi^{(\rm n)},\delta Z^{(\rm n)})\big\|_{C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{0,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)})}\leq\frac{1}{2}\big\|(\delta\Phi^{(\rm n)},\delta Z^{(\rm n)})\big\|_{C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{0,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)})}.\end{split}

This implies that
\mathcal{J}
is a contraction mapping. Hence, there exists a fixed point
(\delta{\varphi},\delta{z})\in C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{0,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)})
such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}(\delta{\varphi}^{(\rm n)},\delta{z}^{(\rm n)})\longrightarrow(\delta{\varphi},\delta{z})\quad\ \ \emph{in}\quad\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0}C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{0,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)})},\end{split}

as
\rm{n}\rightarrow\infty
.

On the other hand, by the estimate (
4.8
) and the compactness of the approximate solutions in
C^{2,\alpha}_{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)})
, there exists a subsequence
\big\{(\delta{\varphi}^{(\rm n_{i})},\delta{z}^{(\rm n_{i})})\big\}^{\infty}_{i=0}
such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}(\delta{\varphi}^{(\rm n_{i})},\delta{z}^{(\rm n_{i})})\longrightarrow(\delta{\varphi}_{*},\delta{z}_{*})\quad\ \ \emph{in}\quad\ C^{2,\alpha}_{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}),\end{split}

as
i\rightarrow\infty
. By the uniqueness of the limit, one has

\displaystyle\begin{split}(\delta{\varphi},\delta{z})=(\delta{\varphi}_{*},\delta{z}_{*})\in C^{2,\alpha}_{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}).\end{split}

Define

\displaystyle\varphi:=\delta\varphi+\underline{\varphi},\ \ \ z:=\delta z+\underline{z}.

Obviously,
(\varphi,z)\in C^{2,\alpha}_{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)})\times C^{1,\alpha}(\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)})
is a weak solution of the problem
(\mathbf{FP})
. ∎

Proof of Theorem
3.3
.

Now we are ready to prove Theorem
3.3
. The existence of solutions to the problem
(\mathbf{FP})

can be derived directly from Lemma
6.1
. Next, for the uniqueness, we take two solutions
(\varphi_{1},z_{1})
and
(\varphi_{2},z_{2})
which satisfy the estimate (
3.125
).
Define

\delta\Phi:=\delta\varphi_{1}-\delta\varphi_{2},\quad\delta Z:=\delta z_{1}-\delta z_{2}.

Then, following the same method as in the proof of the Lemma
6.1
, one can show that

(\delta\Phi,\delta Z)
satisfies the estimate (
6.19
), where
(\delta\Phi^{(\rm n+1)},\delta Z^{(\rm n+1)})
and
(\delta\Phi^{(\rm n)},\delta Z^{(\rm n)})

are replaced by
(\delta\Phi,\delta Z)
. This means that
(\delta\Phi,\delta Z)=(0,0)
.
Thus we have
(\varphi_{1},z_{1})=(\varphi_{2},z_{2})
.
∎

7.
Existence and Uniqueness of the Free Boundary Value Problem
(\mathbf{NP})

Up to now, we have proved that for any
m^{(\rm e)}\in\mathcal{M}_{\sigma}
, the problem
(\mathbf{FP})
admits a unique solution
\big(\varphi,z\big)
satisfying the estimate (
3.125
).
Then, we can define a new number
\hat{m}^{(\rm e)}
by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int^{\hat{m}^{(\rm e)}}_{0}\frac{d\tau}{(\rho^{(\rm e)}u^{(\rm e)})(0,\tau)}=g_{+}(0).\end{split}

(7.1)

By Theorem
3.3
, we know that
\rho^{(\rm e)}u^{(\rm e)}>0
. Then the unique existence of the number
\hat{m}^{(e)}
of equation (
7.1
) follows from the implicit function theorem, and hence the mapping
\mathcal{T}
:

\hat{m}^{(\rm e)}=\mathcal{T}(m^{(\rm e)})
is well defined on
\mathcal{M}_{\sigma}
(see (
3.76
)). Notice that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int^{\underline{m}^{(\rm e)}}_{0}\frac{d\tau}{\underline{\rho}^{(\rm e)}\underline{u}^{(\rm e)}}=1,\end{split}

then

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int^{\hat{m}^{(\rm e)}}_{0}\frac{d\tau}{(\rho^{(\rm e)}u^{(\rm e)})(0,\tau)}-\int^{\underline{m}^{(\rm e)}}_{0}\frac{d\tau}{\underline{\rho}^{(\rm e)}\underline{u}^{(\rm e)}}=g_{+}(0)-1,\end{split}

i.e.,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int^{\hat{m}^{(\rm e)}}_{\underline{m}^{(\rm e)}}\frac{d\tau}{(\rho^{(\rm e)}u^{(\rm e)})(0,\tau)}=\int^{\underline{m}^{(\rm e)}}_{0}\Big(\frac{1}{(\rho^{(\rm e)}u^{(\rm e)})(0,\tau)}-\frac{1}{\underline{\rho}^{(\rm e)}\underline{u}^{(\rm e)}}\Big)d\tau+g_{+}(0)-1.\end{split}

Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{split}|\hat{m}^{(\rm e)}-\underline{m}^{(\rm e)}|&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\big\|\varphi-\underline{\varphi}\big\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\|g_{+}-1\|_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Gamma}_{+}}\Big)\leq\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}},\end{split}

where the constant
\mathcal{C}>0
depends only on
\underline{U}
,
L
and
\alpha
.
Hence, we can choose
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small such that
\mathcal{C}\tilde{\epsilon}<\sigma
.
Then the mapping
\mathcal{T}
is from
\mathcal{M}_{\sigma}
to
\mathcal{M}_{\sigma}
.

Next, we will show that the mapping
\mathcal{T}
is contractive. Give two numbers
m^{(\rm e)}_{A},m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\in\mathcal{M}_{\sigma}
and the corresponding solutions are

(\varphi_{A},z_{A})
and
(\varphi_{B},z_{B})
, which are defined respectively in the domains
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}_{A}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{A}

and
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}_{B}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{B}
. Let

\displaystyle\pi_{A}:\ \left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\breve{\xi}=\xi,\quad\breve{\eta}=\frac{\eta}{m^{(\rm e)}_{A}},&\ (\xi,\eta)\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}_{A},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{\xi}=\xi,\quad\breve{\eta}=\eta,&\ (\xi,\eta)\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{A},\\[5.0pt]
\end{array}\right.

and

\displaystyle\pi_{B}:\ \left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\breve{\xi}=\xi,\quad\breve{\eta}=\frac{\eta}{m^{(\rm e)}_{B}},&\ \ \ \ (\xi,\eta)\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}_{B},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{\xi}=\xi,\quad\breve{\eta}=\eta,&\ \ \ \ (\xi,\eta)\in\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{B}.\end{array}\right.

Then, under the coordinate transformation
\pi_{j}
, the domain
\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}_{j}\cup\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}_{j}(j=A,B)
is transformed into the same domain:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}\cup\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}=\big\{(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}):0<\breve{\xi}<1,\ 0<\breve{\eta}<1\big\}\cup\big\{(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}):0<\breve{\xi}<L,\ -m^{(\rm h)}<\breve{\eta}<0\big\}.\end{split}

The boundaries
\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{{\rm in},j}
,
\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{{\rm ex},j}
,
\tilde{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{{\rm in},j}
,
\tilde{\Gamma}_{+,j}
,
\tilde{\Gamma}_{{\rm cd},j}
and
\tilde{\Gamma}_{-,j}
,
(j=A,B)
become

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}=\big\{(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}):0<\breve{\eta}<1,\breve{\xi}=0\big\},\ \breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex}=\big\{(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}):0<\breve{\eta}<1,\breve{\xi}=L\big\},\\[5.0pt]
&\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}=\big\{(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}):-m^{(\rm h)}<\breve{\eta}<0,\breve{\xi}=0\big\},\ \breve{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}=\big\{(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}):0<\breve{\xi}<L,\breve{\eta}=0\big\},\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\breve{\Gamma}_{+}=\big\{(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}):0<\breve{\xi}<L,\breve{\eta}=1\big\},\ \breve{\Gamma}_{-}=\big\{(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}):0<\breve{\xi}<L,\breve{\eta}=-m^{(\rm h)}\big\}.\end{split}

For
j=A,\ B
, denote
\breve{D}^{(j)}=\big(\breve{\partial}^{(j)}_{1},\ \breve{\partial}^{(j)}_{2}\big)=\big(\partial_{\breve{\xi}},\ \frac{\partial_{\breve{\eta}}}{m^{(\rm e)}_{j}}\big)
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\breve{\varphi}_{j}(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta})=\varphi_{j}(\breve{\xi},m^{(\rm e)}_{j}\breve{\eta}),\ \ \big(\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,j},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,j}\big)(\breve{\eta})=\big(\tilde{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0},\tilde{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0}\big)(\breve{\xi},m^{(\rm e)}_{j}\breve{\eta}),\end{split}

(7.8)

for
(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta})\in\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}
, and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\breve{z}_{j}(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta})=z_{j}(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}),\ \ \big(\breve{B}^{(\rm h)}_{0,j},\breve{S}^{(\rm h)}_{0,j}\big)(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta})=\big(\tilde{B}^{(\rm h)}_{0},\tilde{S}^{(\rm h)}_{0}\big)(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}),\end{split}

(7.9)

for
(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta})\in\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}
.
Then,
\breve{\varphi}_{j}
and
\breve{z}_{j}
,
(j=A,B)
satisfy

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\partial_{\breve{\xi}}\mathcal{N}_{1}\big(\breve{D}^{(j)}\breve{\varphi}_{j};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,j},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,j}\big)+m^{(\rm e)}_{j}\partial_{\breve{\eta}}\mathcal{N}_{2}\big(\breve{D}^{(j)}\breve{\varphi}_{j};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,j},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,j}\big)=0,&\ \ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{\breve{\xi}}\breve{z}_{j}+\textrm{diag}(\breve{\lambda}_{+,j},\breve{\lambda}_{-,j})\partial_{\breve{\eta}}\breve{z}_{j}=0,&\ \ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{p}^{(\rm e)}_{j}=\tilde{p}_{0}(m^{(\rm e)}_{j}\breve{\eta}),&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{\breve{\xi}}\breve{\varphi}_{j}=\tilde{\omega}_{\rm e}(m^{(\rm e)}_{j}\breve{\eta}),&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{z}_{j}=z_{0}(\breve{\eta}),&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{\breve{\xi}}\breve{\varphi}_{j}=g_{+}(\breve{\xi}),&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}_{+},\\[5.0pt]
\partial_{\breve{\xi}}\breve{\varphi}_{j}=\breve{\omega}^{(\rm h)}_{j},\ \breve{p}^{(\rm e)}_{j}=\breve{p}^{(\rm h)}_{j},&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{z}_{-,j}+\breve{z}_{+,j}=2\arctan g^{\prime}_{-}(\breve{\xi}),&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}_{-},\end{array}\right.

where
\breve{\lambda}_{\pm}=\lambda_{\pm}\big(\breve{z};\breve{B}^{(\rm h)}_{0},\breve{S}^{(\rm h)}_{0}\big)
and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\breve{\omega}^{(\rm h)}_{j}=\tilde{\omega}^{(\rm h)}_{j}(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}),\quad\breve{p}^{(\rm e)}_{j}=\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}\big(\breve{D}^{(j)}\breve{\varphi}_{j};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,j},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,j}\big),\quad\breve{p}^{(\rm h)}_{j}=\tilde{p}^{(\rm h)}_{j}(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta}).\end{split}

(7.19)

Set
\breve{\varphi}_{AB}=\breve{\varphi}_{A}-\breve{\varphi}_{B}
and
\breve{z}_{AB}=\breve{z}_{A}-\breve{z}_{B}
.
Then,
\breve{\varphi}_{AB}
and
\breve{z}_{AB}
satisfy the following initial boundary value problem:

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}\sum_{i,j=1,2}\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{i}\big(\breve{a}_{ij}(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta})\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{j}\breve{\varphi}_{AB}\big)=\breve{b},&\ \ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{1}\breve{z}_{AB}+\textrm{diag}(\breve{\lambda}_{+,A},\breve{\lambda}_{-,A})\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{2}\breve{z}_{AB}\\[5.0pt]
\ \ \qquad\qquad=\textrm{diag}\big(\breve{\lambda}_{+,B}-\breve{\lambda}_{+,A},\breve{\lambda}_{-,B}-\breve{\lambda}_{-,A}\big)\breve{\partial}_{2}\breve{z}^{(A)}_{-,B},&\ \ \ \mbox{in}\ \ \ \breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{\varphi}_{AB}=\breve{g}_{0,AB}(\breve{\eta}),&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{1}\breve{\varphi}_{AB}=\tilde{\omega}_{\rm e}(m^{(\rm e)}_{A}\breve{\eta})-\tilde{\omega}_{\rm e}(m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\breve{\eta}),&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{z}_{AB}=0,&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{\varphi}_{AB}=\breve{g}_{+,AB}(\breve{\xi}),&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}_{+},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{z}_{-,AB}+\breve{z}_{+,AB}=2\arctan(\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{1}\breve{\varphi}_{AB}+\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{1}\breve{\varphi}_{B})-2\arctan\breve{\partial}^{(B)}_{1}\breve{\varphi}_{B},&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{z}_{-,AB}-\breve{z}_{+,AB}=2\breve{\beta}_{\rm cd,1}\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{1}\breve{\varphi}_{AB}+2\breve{\beta}_{\rm cd,2}\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{2}\breve{\varphi}_{AB}+2\breve{c}_{\rm cd}(\breve{\xi}),&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}_{\rm cd},\\[5.0pt]
\breve{z}_{-,AB}+\breve{z}_{+,AB}=0,&\ \ \ \mbox{on}\ \ \ \breve{\Gamma}_{-},\end{array}\right.

where
\breve{a}_{ij}(\breve{\xi},\breve{\eta})=a_{ij}\big(\breve{D}^{(A)}\breve{\varphi}_{A},\breve{D}^{(A)}\breve{\varphi}_{B},\breve{\eta}\big),
and

\breve{\beta}_{0,\ell}=\beta_{\rm 0,\ell}\big(\breve{D}^{(A)}\breve{\varphi}_{A},\breve{D}^{(A)}\breve{\varphi}_{B},\breve{\eta}\big),\quad\breve{\beta}_{\rm cd,\ell}=\beta_{\rm cd,\ell}\big(\breve{D}^{(A)}\breve{\varphi}_{A},\breve{D}^{(A)}\breve{\varphi}_{B},\breve{\eta}\big)

for
\ell=1,2
;

\displaystyle\begin{split}\breve{b}=&\sum_{\ell=1,2}\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{\ell}\Big(\mathcal{N}_{\ell}\big(\breve{D}_{B}\delta\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A}\big)-\mathcal{N}_{\ell}\big(\breve{D}_{A}\delta\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A}\big)\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&+\sum_{\ell=1,2}\breve{\partial}^{(B)}_{\ell}\Big(\mathcal{N}_{\ell}\big(\breve{D}_{B}\delta\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,B},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,B}\big)-\mathcal{N}_{\ell}\big(\breve{D}_{B}\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A}\big)\Big)\\[5.0pt]
&+\big(\breve{\partial}^{(B)}_{2}-\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{2}\big)\mathcal{N}_{2}\big(\breve{D}_{B}\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A}\big),\end{split}

\breve{c}_{\rm cd}(\breve{\xi})=\Theta\Big(p^{(\rm e)}\big(\breve{D}_{A}\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A}\big);\breve{B}^{(\rm h)}_{0},\breve{S}^{(\rm h)}_{0}\Big)-\Theta\Big(p^{(\rm e)}\big(\breve{D}_{B}\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,B},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,B}\big);\breve{B}^{(\rm h)}_{0},\breve{S}^{(\rm h)}_{0}\Big),

\displaystyle\begin{split}\breve{g}_{0,AB}(\check{\eta})&=\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\int^{\check{\eta}}_{0}\Big(\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}_{0}(m^{(\rm e)}_{A}\mu)-\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}_{0}(m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\mu)-\underline{\tilde{p}}^{(\rm e)}_{0}(m^{(\rm e)}_{A}\mu)+\underline{\tilde{p}}^{(\rm e)}_{0}(m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\mu)\Big)d\mu\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ -\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\check{\eta}}_{0}\big(\check{\beta}^{(A)}_{0,j}-\underline{\beta}_{0,j}\big)\check{\partial}^{(A)}_{j}\check{\varphi}_{AB}d\mu\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ -\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\check{\eta}}_{0}\big(\check{\beta}^{(A)}_{0,j}-\check{\beta}^{(B)}_{0,j}\big)\check{\partial}^{(A)}_{j}\delta\check{\varphi}_{B}d\mu\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ -\frac{1}{\underline{\beta}_{0,2}}\sum_{j=1,2}\int^{\check{\eta}}_{0}\big(\check{\beta}^{(B)}_{0,j}-\underline{\beta}_{0,j}\big)\Big(\check{\partial}^{(A)}_{j}-\check{\partial}^{(A)}_{j}\Big)\delta\check{\varphi}_{B}d\mu,\end{split}

and
\breve{g}_{+,AB}(\xi)=\breve{g}_{0,AB}(1)
.
Here
\check{\beta}^{(k)}_{0,j}
,
(k=A,B,j=1,2)
are given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\breve{\beta}^{(k)}_{0,1}:=\int^{1}_{0}\partial_{\breve{\partial}^{(k)}_{1}\breve{\varphi}}\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}\big(\breve{D}^{(k)}\underline{\varphi}+\nu\check{D}^{(k)}\delta\check{\varphi}_{k};\breve{\tilde{B}}^{(\rm e)}_{0,k},\breve{\tilde{S}}^{(\rm e)}_{0,k}\big)d\nu,\\[5.0pt]
\breve{\beta}^{(k)}_{0,2}:=\int^{1}_{0}\partial_{\breve{\partial}^{(k)}_{2}\breve{\varphi}}\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}\big(\breve{D}^{(k)}\underline{\varphi}+\nu\breve{D}^{(k)}\delta\breve{\varphi}_{k};\breve{\tilde{B}}^{(\rm e)}_{0,k},\breve{\tilde{S}}^{(\rm e)}_{0,k}\big)d\nu,\end{split}

and
\delta\breve{\varphi}_{k}=\breve{\varphi}_{k}-\underline{\varphi}
for
k=A,B
.

By direct computations, we can choose
\sigma_{0}>0
which depends only on the background state
\tilde{\underline{U}}

such that for any
\sigma\in(0,\sigma_{0})
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\breve{b}\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}&\leq\sum_{\ell=1,2}\Big\|\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{\ell}\Big(\mathcal{N}_{\ell}\big(\breve{D}_{B}\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A}\big)-\mathcal{N}_{\ell}\big(\breve{D}_{A}\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A}\big)\Big)\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ +\sum_{\ell=1,2}\Big\|\breve{\partial}^{(B)}_{\ell}\Big(\mathcal{N}_{\ell}\big(\breve{D}_{B}\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,B},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,B}\big)-\mathcal{N}_{\ell}\big(\breve{D}_{B}\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A}\big)\Big)\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ +\Big\|\big(\breve{\partial}^{(B)}_{2}-\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{2}\big)\mathcal{N}_{2}\big(\breve{D}_{B}\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A}\big)\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|\delta\breve{\varphi}_{B}\|^{{(-1-\alpha,\tilde{\Sigma}^{(\rm e)}\setminus\{\mathcal{O}\})}}_{2,\alpha;\tilde{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\big\|(\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0}-\underline{B}^{(\rm e)},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0}-\underline{S}^{(\rm e)})\big\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}\Big)\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\big|\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\big|,\end{split}

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\breve{c}_{\rm cd}(\breve{\xi})\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq 2\bigg\|\Theta\Big(p^{(\rm e)}\big(\breve{D}_{A}\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,A}\big);\breve{B}^{(\rm h)}_{0},\breve{S}^{(\rm h)}_{0}\Big)-\Theta\Big(p^{(\rm e)}\big(\breve{D}_{B}\breve{\varphi}_{B};\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0,B},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0,B}\big);\breve{B}^{(\rm h)}_{0},\breve{S}^{(\rm h)}_{0}\Big)\bigg\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|\delta\breve{\varphi}_{B}\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\big\|(\breve{B}^{(\rm e)}_{0}-\underline{B}^{(\rm e)},\breve{S}^{(\rm e)}_{0}-\underline{S}^{(\rm e)})\big\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}\Big)\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\big|\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\big|,\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\breve{g}_{0,AB}\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}+\|\breve{g}_{+,AB}\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}_{+}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big\|\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}_{0}(m^{(\rm e)}_{A}\breve{\eta})-\tilde{p}^{(\rm e)}_{0}(m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\breve{\eta})-\underline{\tilde{p}}^{(\rm e)}_{0}(m^{(\rm e)}_{A}\breve{\eta})+\underline{\tilde{p}}^{(\rm e)}_{0}(m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\breve{\eta})\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ +\Big\|\big(\check{\beta}^{(A)}_{0,j}-\underline{\beta}_{0,j}\big)\check{\partial}^{(A)}_{j}\check{\varphi}_{AB}\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}+\Big\|\big(\check{\beta}^{(A)}_{0,j}-\check{\beta}^{(B)}_{0,j}\big)\check{\partial}^{(A)}_{j}\delta\check{\varphi}_{B}\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}\\[5.0pt]
&\ \ \ +\Big\|\big(\check{\beta}^{(B)}_{0,j}-\underline{\beta}_{0,j}\big)\Big(\check{\partial}^{(A)}_{j}-\check{\partial}^{(A)}_{j}\Big)\delta\check{\varphi}_{B}\Big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}\Big(\|\delta\breve{\varphi}_{B}\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\|\breve{p}^{(\rm e)}_{0}-\underline{p}^{(\rm e)}\|_{1,\alpha;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}}\Big)\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\big|\\[5.0pt]
&\leq\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\big|.\end{split}

Here, the constant
\mathcal{C}>0
depends only on the background state
\underline{U}
and
\alpha
.
Now from the similar argument in the proof of Theorem
3.3
, due to (
3.124
), if
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
is sufficiently small, then in
\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}
there exists a constant
\mathcal{C}>0
depending only on the background state
\tilde{\underline{U}}
,
L
and
\alpha
such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\breve{z}_{-,AB}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\leq\mathcal{C}\big\|\partial_{\breve{\eta}}\breve{z}_{-,B}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\big\|\breve{z}_{AB}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\leq\mathcal{C}\tilde{\epsilon}\big\|\breve{z}_{AB}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}.\end{split}

Next, we can solve
\breve{\varphi}_{AB}
in
\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}
with the following boundary condition on
\breve{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}
:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\tilde{\breve{\beta}}_{\rm cd,1}\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{\breve{\xi}}\breve{\varphi}_{AB}+\tilde{\breve{\beta}}_{\rm cd,2}\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{\breve{\eta}}\breve{\varphi}_{AB}=\tilde{\breve{c}}_{\rm cd}(\breve{\xi}),\end{split}

where

\displaystyle\begin{split}\tilde{\breve{\beta}}_{\rm cd,1}=\breve{\beta}_{\rm cd,1}+\int^{1}_{0}\frac{d\varsigma}{1+(\varsigma\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{\xi}\breve{\varphi}_{AB}+\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{\xi}\breve{\varphi}_{B})^{2}},\quad\tilde{\breve{\beta}}_{\rm cd,2}=\breve{\beta}_{\rm cd,2},\end{split}

and

\tilde{\breve{c}}_{\rm cd}(\breve{\xi})=-\breve{c}_{\rm cd}(\breve{\xi})+\arctan\breve{\partial}^{(B)}_{\xi}\breve{\varphi}_{B}-\arctan\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{\xi}\breve{\varphi}_{B}+\breve{z}_{-,AB}
.

Thus, by choosing a constant
\alpha_{0}\in(0,1)
depending only on
\tilde{\underline{U}}
and
L
such that for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{0})
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&{\big\|\breve{\varphi}_{AB}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\mathcal{C}\bigg(\|\breve{b}\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\|\breve{g}_{0,AB}\|_{1,\alpha;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm in}}}+\|\breve{g}_{+,AB}\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}_{+}}+\big\|\tilde{\omega}_{\rm e}(m^{(\rm e)}_{A}\breve{\eta})-\tilde{\omega}_{\rm e}(m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\breve{\eta})\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm e)}_{\rm ex}}+\|\tilde{\breve{c}}_{\rm cd}\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\bigg)\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\big|+\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\Big(\big\|\breve{z}_{AB}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\big\|\breve{\varphi}_{AB}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\Big).\end{split}

Finally, we further estimate
\breve{z}_{+,AB}
by using the boundary condition on
\breve{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}
and the initial condition
on
\breve{\Gamma}^{(\rm h)}_{\rm in}
to establish

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|\breve{z}_{+,AB}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\mathcal{C}\bigg(\big\|2\breve{\beta}_{\rm cd,1}\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{\breve{\xi}}\breve{\varphi}_{AB}+2\breve{\beta}_{\rm cd,2}\breve{\partial}^{(A)}_{\breve{\eta}}\breve{\varphi}_{AB}+2\breve{c}_{\rm cd}(\breve{\xi})\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}+\|\breve{z}_{-,AB}\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Gamma}_{\rm cd}}\bigg)+\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\big\|\breve{z}_{AB}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\mathcal{C}\big\|\breve{\varphi}_{AB}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\mathcal{C}\big\|\breve{c}_{\rm cd}(\breve{\xi})\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\big\|\breve{z}_{AB}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\\[5.0pt]
\leq&\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\big|+\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\Big(\big\|\breve{z}_{AB}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\big\|\breve{\varphi}_{AB}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}\Big).\end{split}

Hence,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\breve{\varphi}_{AB}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\big\|\breve{z}_{AB}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\leq\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\Big(\|\breve{z}_{AB}\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}+\big\|\breve{\varphi}_{AB}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}|\Big),\end{split}

where constant
\mathcal{C}>0
depends only on
\underline{U}
,
\alpha
and
L
. Thus, for
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
sufficiently small, it follows that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big\|\breve{\varphi}_{AB}\big\|_{1,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}+\big\|\breve{z}_{AB}\big\|_{0,\alpha;\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm h)}}\leq\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}|.\end{split}

(7.31)

Therefore, by (
7.1
), we have that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int^{m^{(\rm e)}_{B}}_{m^{(\rm e)}_{A}}\frac{d\tau}{\rho^{(\rm e)}_{B}u^{(\rm e)}_{B}}=\int^{m^{(\rm e)}_{A}}_{0}\Big(\frac{1}{\rho^{(\rm e)}_{A}u^{(\rm e)}_{A}}-\frac{1}{\rho^{(\rm e)}_{B}u^{(\rm e)}_{B}}\Big)d\tau.\end{split}

(7.32)

Then it follows from (
7.31
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{B}-m^{(\rm e)}_{A}\big|\leq\mathcal{C}\big\|\breve{\varphi}_{AB}\big\|_{1,\alpha;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\breve{\Omega}^{(\rm e)}}}\leq\mathcal{C}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\epsilon}}\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\big|.\end{split}

Let
\tilde{\epsilon}>0
be sufficiently small such that
\mathcal{C}\tilde{\epsilon}\leq\frac{1}{2}
. Then

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{B}-m^{(\rm e)}_{A}\big|\leq\frac{1}{2}\big|m^{(\rm e)}_{A}-m^{(\rm e)}_{B}\big|,\end{split}

which implies that the mapping
\mathcal{T}
is contractive.

Based on the above argument, we can prove Theorem
3.2
easily.

Proof of Theorem
3.2
.

The existence and uniqueness of the solution to
(\mathbf{NP})
follow from the Theorem
3.3
and the fact that the mapping
\mathcal{T}
is contractive.
∎
